### [1] [Integral zeros of Krawtchouk polynomials and quantum entanglements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08518)
*Jaeseong Heo,Young-Hoon Kiem,Chul-hee Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文解决了二元Krawtchouk多项式整数零点问题中的三个非对称参数族，并将其与量子信息中满足range criterion的子空间对的存在性相联系，剩余情形为稀疏零点且每个m≥5仅有有限多个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Heo与Kiem(2019)将二元Krawtchouk多项式（由四元组(m,n,k,ℓ)参数化，m≤n且k≤ℓ）的整数零点问题归结为量子信息论中满足range criterion的子空间对的存在性问题，但仅解决了对称情形k=ℓ。本文旨在解决非对称情形。

Method: 对三个具体的非对称参数族给出完整求解：(m,n,k,ℓ)=(3,t²−1,t(t−1)/2,t(t+1)/2)，t≥2；(4,3t²+2t−1,(3t²−t)/2,(3t²+5t+2)/2)，t≥2；(4,3s²+4s,(3s²+s)/2,(3s²+7s+4)/2)，s≥1。通过分析这些族的整数零点结构完成求解。

Result: 三个非对称参数族的整数零点问题均被完全解决；其余情况仅存在零星零点(sporadic zeros)，且对每个m≥5仅有有限多个。

Conclusion: 结合Heo–Kiem的对称情形结果，非对称情形的主要无穷族已被刻画，剩余的整数零点为有限稀疏情形，从而基本完成了该问题的分类。

Abstract: In Heo and Kiem (2019), the authors raised a problem relating integral zeros of binary Krawtchouk polynomials, parametrized by quadruples $(m,n,k,\ell)$ with $m\le n$ and $k\le \ell$, to the existence of pairs of subspaces satisfying the range criterion in quantum information theory, and settled the symmetric case $k=\ell$. In this paper, we solve the problem for the following three asymmetric families:
  $(m,n,k,\ell)=(3,t^2-1,t(t-1)/2,t(t+1)/2)$, $t\ge2$;
  $(m,n,k,\ell)=(4,3t^2+2t-1,(3t^2-t)/2,(3t^2+5t+2)/2)$, $t\ge2$;
  $(m,n,k,\ell)=(4,3s^2+4s,(3s^2+s)/2,(3s^2+7s+4)/2)$, $s\ge1$.
  The remaining cases are sporadic zeros, with only finitely many for each $m\ge5$.

</details>


### [2] [Thermodynamics of Speed and Efficiency with Quantum Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08564)
*Yue Liu,Ryotaro Honma,Ruicheng Bao,Tan Van Vu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对连续监测、跳跃反馈的开放量子动力学，推导了有限时间电流—耗散关系，并由此建立量子速度极限与稳态信息引擎的功率—效率权衡，量化了反馈对状态变换与引擎性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速热力学操作通常伴随不可逆耗散，导致速度与热力学代价之间存在权衡；而量子反馈如何从根本上重塑这一权衡尚不清楚，缺乏定量刻画。

Method: 对带跳跃反馈的连续监测开放量子动力学推导有限时间电流—耗散关系；基于该基本关系导出量子速度极限，以及稳态信息引擎的功率—效率权衡，并将反馈效应分解为信息熵贡献与直接动力学贡献。

Result: 得到有限时间电流—耗散关系、量子速度极限及功率—效率权衡；在有限时间信息擦除中实现广义Landauer界，在集体引擎的大系统极限下于有限功率下趋近单位信息效率。

Conclusion: 结果为反馈控制下的快速、低耗散量子热力学提供了定量基础，并表明反馈可通过信息熵与动力学两条途径重塑速度—代价权衡。

Abstract: Rapid thermodynamic operations generally entail irreversible dissipation, giving rise to trade-offs between operating speed and thermodynamic cost. Yet, how quantum feedback fundamentally reshapes these trade-offs remains elusive. Here, we derive a finite-time current--dissipation relation for continuously monitored open quantum dynamics with jump feedback. From this fundamental relation, we establish a quantum speed limit and a power-efficiency trade-off for stationary information engines, identifying how feedback affects state transformations and the performance of engines through both informational entropy and direct dynamical contributions. Our results provide a quantitative foundation for fast, low-dissipation quantum thermodynamics in the presence of feedback control, exemplified by attaining the generalized Landauer bound in finite-time information erasure and approaching unit information efficiency at finite power in the large-system limit of a collective engine.

</details>


### [3] [Magic Is in the Eye of the Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08586)
*Zhi-Heng Shen,Zhewei Yin,Hao-Lin Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对比了两种量子比特下四种"魔法"（非稳定子性）度量：稳定子Rényi熵与三种基于稳定子态优化的度量（最小/最大相对熵、对数自由鲁棒性），证明各自的最大魔法态并指出其依赖度量；在Møller散射最小魔法提取弱混合角时，最小相对熵给出能量依赖值，而SRE、最大相对熵与对数自由鲁棒性给出sin²θ_W=1/4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能与核物理中魔法应用几乎只用稳定子Rényi熵，但魔法并无唯一度量，因此需考察不同度量是否导致不同的物理结论。

Method: 在两量子比特系统中，将SRE与三种优化型度量（最小相对熵、最大相对熵、对数自由鲁棒性）逐一比较，并给出各度量最大魔法态的严格证明；进一步用各度量计算Møller散射最小魔法对应的弱混合角。

Result: 四种度量的最大魔法态各不相同（依赖度量）；弱混合角提取中，最小相对熵给出能量依赖的sin²θ_W，而最大相对熵与对数自由鲁棒性在各能量下均给出sin²θ_W=1/4。

Conclusion: 差异源于优化型度量的非解析结构；由极值化魔法得出的物理结论可能依赖所选度量，需谨慎对待单一度量的推广。

Abstract: Magic, or nonstabilizerness, is the quantum resource behind universal quantum computation. Its applications in high-energy and nuclear physics rely almost exclusively on the stabilizer Rényi entropy (SRE), although magic has no unique measure. For two qubits, we compare the SRE with three measures defined through optimizations over stabilizer states: the min- and max-relative entropies of magic and the logarithmic free robustness. We determine the maximal-magic states of all four measures, with exact proofs, and find that they depend on the measure. In the determination of the weak mixing angle from minimal magic in Møller scattering, the min-relative entropy gives an energy-dependent value that differs from that of the SRE, whereas the max-relative entropy and the logarithmic free robustness give $\sin^2θ_W=1/4$ at all energies. We trace this difference to the non-analytic structure of the optimization-based measures. Physical conclusions drawn from extremizing magic can thus depend on the measure.

</details>


### [4] [Universality in Quantum Many-Body Spin Ratchets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08590)
*Andrea Azzali,Marko Ljubotina*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了量子多体自旋棘轮的普适漂移速度在任意时刻精确成立，仅需平移不变性与U(1)对称性；并推广至平均平移不变（随机）情形，同时说明破坏平移不变性会立即导致偏离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明可积、SU(2)对称的量子自旋棘轮漂移速度具有普适性，并对非可积情形提出猜想；作者希望给出严格证明并厘清该普适性成立所需的最弱对称性条件。

Method: 针对作用于偶数/奇数格点具有两类自旋的交错自旋链量子线路（破坏宇称对称、表现手性输运），在仅有平移不变性与U(1)对称性的条件下对漂移速度作解析证明；并通过在随机（无序）情形下推导同一结果，证明平均平移不变性即已充分。

Result: 证明了该普适漂移在所有时刻精确成立；在线路仅平均平移不变时同样成立；并证明一旦破坏平移不变性，漂移立即偏离该普适预测值。

Conclusion: 普适漂移速度的根源在于平移不变性与U(1)对称性，而非可积性或SU(2)对称性；该结论对非可积体系及平均平移不变情形均适用，且平移不变性是必要前提。

Abstract: We consider a class of quantum circuits, known as quantum many-body spin ratchets, which act on alternating spin chains with two distinct spin types on even and odd sites. This setup breaks parity symmetry and exhibits chiral transport. It has been shown that the drift velocity in the case of integrable, SU(2)-symmetric quantum spin ratchets is universal, and conjectured that the same holds in the non-integrable case. Here, we prove that this universal drift is exact at all times, requiring only translation invariance and U(1) symmetry. Furthermore, we prove that it is sufficient for the circuit to be translation-invariant on average by deriving the same result in the random case. Finally, we demonstrate that breaking translation invariance immediately results in deviations from this universal prediction.

</details>


### [5] [Exploring topological signatures of quantum Fisher information scaling in graph states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08593)
*Majid Hassani,Patrick Emonts,Jordi Tura*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文数值研究图的单纯复形Betti数是否刻画图态量子Fisher信息超越标准量子极限(SQL)的行为，结论是Betti数可导向有测量价值的图但单独不能决定QFI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图态的QFI有闭式表达（计数具有相同邻域的点对），但哪些全局结构性质决定QFI是否超越SQL尚不清楚；作者试图检验拓扑不变量（clique复形的Betti数）能否刻画这一行为。

Method: 两种互补数值策略：(1) 在平衡完全多部图上随机加边或删边，该图族同时具备超SQL的QFI与单个非零高阶Betti数；(2) 从Erdős–Rényi随机图出发，用Monte Carlo搜索最大化选定Betti数。

Result: 加边最终同时破坏测量优势与非平凡拓扑；删边时高阶Betti数在QFI已降至SQL后仍长期存在。Betti数最大化搜索把图推向近极端多部结构，其QFI显著超越SQL。

Conclusion: 拓扑优化是获得有测量价值图态的可行途径，但Betti数本身并不决定QFI，二者并非一一对应。

Abstract: Graph states are a versatile resource for quantum metrology, and their quantum Fisher information (QFI) has a closed-form expression in terms of the underlying graph: it counts pairs of vertices with identical neighborhoods. It remains unclear, however, which global structural properties of a graph determine whether its QFI exceeds the standard quantum limit (SQL). Here we ask whether topological invariants, namely the Betti numbers of the graph's clique complex, capture this behavior, and we address the question numerically with two complementary approaches. First, we randomly add or delete edges in balanced complete multipartite graphs, which combine a QFI beyond the SQL with a single nonvanishing higher Betti number. Edge addition eventually destroys both the metrological advantage and the nontrivial topology. Under edge deletion, by contrast, higher Betti numbers persist well after the QFI has dropped to the SQL. Second, we start from Erdős--Rényi random graphs, whose QFI does not typically exceed the SQL, and maximize a chosen Betti number with a Monte Carlo search. The search drives the graphs toward near-extremal multipartite structures whose QFI lies well beyond the SQL. Our results show that topological optimization offers a route to metrologically useful graph states, but also that Betti numbers alone do not determine the QFI.

</details>


### [6] [Harnessing dimensional leakage for continuous-variable randomness certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08607)
*Moisés Alves,Tailan S. Sarubi,Vitor L. Sena,Alexandre B. Tacla,Rafael Chaves*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出半器件无关协议中严格的希尔伯特空间维度假设难以实现，借助“almost-qudit”框架量化泄漏，并证明对完整输入系综取平均可缓解泄漏导致的随机性认证失效，从而用低压缩单模高斯态与零差探测实现非零随机性认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半器件无关量子协议通常假设严格的希尔伯特空间维度，而真实实现（尤其是无限维光场）难以满足；almost-qudit框架虽可量化目标子空间之外的泄漏，但泄漏会严重破坏固定制备与测量输入下的随机性认证。

Method: 在almost-qudit框架下量化超越目标子空间的量子态泄漏，考察固定输入时的随机性认证脆弱性，进而分析在完整输入系综上取平均对泄漏容忍度的影响，并应用于低压缩单模高斯态加零差探测的连续变量方案。

Result: 对完整输入系综取平均可缓解泄漏带来的脆弱性并扩展可容忍的泄漏范围；该鲁棒性提升使低压缩单模高斯态与零差探测能够实现非零随机性认证。

Conclusion: 输入系综平均提升了almost-qudit框架对泄漏的鲁棒性，给出一个实验上易实现的连续变量半器件无关随机性认证方案。

Abstract: Semi-device-independent quantum protocols often rely on strict Hilbert-space dimension assumptions that are difficult to satisfy in realistic implementations, particularly with infinite-dimensional optical fields. The almost-qudit framework addresses this issue by quantifying the leakage of quantum states beyond a target subspace. Although such leakage can severely compromise randomness certification for fixed preparation and measurement inputs, we show that averaging over the full input ensemble can mitigate this vulnerability and extend the range of tolerable leakage. This enhanced robustness enables nonzero randomness certification using low-squeezing single-mode Gaussian states and homodyne detection, thus offering an experimentally accessible continuous-variable implementation.

</details>


### [7] [Scalable Unitary State-Encoding for Generic Large Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09760)
*Vittorio Pagni,Peter K. Schuhmacher,Michael Felderer,Michael Epping,Kevin Lively*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出低双比特门开销、可在电路深度与编码保真度间权衡的确定性可扩展振幅编码方法，核心是将全控多路复用电路化简为单/双比特门，并在IQM硬件上验证了高保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将数据高效编码到量子态是许多量子计算协议的基础瓶颈，现有默认态制备方法门深度过大，使应用在第一步计算前就退相干为噪声。

Method: 提出两种互补算法将全控多路复用器电路化简为单、双比特门：其一把冗余控制门消除形式化为布尔逻辑最小化问题；其二利用单个辅助比特，通过门角度的Walsh-Hadamard谱将多控旋转门分解为单、双比特操作序列。方法无需振幅放大或中电路测量，并可借助张量网络编码超出经典内存的任意复数据或可查询函数。

Result: 在广泛函数类、全连接图下系统表征总深度随规模与目标保真度的关系；两法混合相较Qiskit默认态制备方法在同等保真度下电路深度提升可达数个数量级；在方形格点连接的IQM硬件上以极少噪声缓解即获得优异保真度。

Conclusion: 大幅降低编码门深度，使原本在首次计算前即被噪声淹没的应用可在当前及近期硬件上实验执行，缩短实现稳健量子实用性的时间尺度。

Abstract: Efficient encoding of data into quantum states remains a fundamental bottleneck in many quantum computing protocols, which would otherwise offer advantages over their classical counterparts. Here, we introduce deterministic and scalable amplitude-encoding methods with low two-qubit gate costs and tunable trade-offs between circuit depth and encoding fidelity. Our approaches require no amplitude amplification or mid-circuit measurement and are capable of encoding arbitrary complex data or queryable functions by using tensor-networks to go beyond what can be held in classical memory. Our principle contributions are two complementary algorithms for simplifying fully controlled multiplexer circuits to one- and two-qubit gates. In the first, we formally map the elimination of redundant gate controls to a Boolean logic minimization problem, while in the second we utilize a single ancilla qubit to map the multi-controlled rotation gates to a sequence of one- and two-qubit operations via the Walsh-Hadamard spectrum of the gate angles. We benchmark these methods across a broad range of function classes, characterizing their total depth in an all-to-all connectivity graph against system size and target fidelity. Hybridizing our methods gives up to orders of magnitude improvement in circuit depth at equivalent state fidelity compared to the default state-preparation method in the Qiskit package. We furthermore demonstrate our method on IQM hardware with a square lattice connectivity, finding excellent fidelity, even with minimal noise mitigation. By significantly reducing gate depth for encoding, this work enables the experimental execution of applications on current and near-term hardware which would otherwise be reduced to noise before the first calculation step, shortening the time horizon to robust quantum utility.

</details>


### [8] [Quantum-enabled measurement of gravitational forces at the milligram scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08619)
*Matthew Herbst,Ewa Rej,Joe Depellette,Richa Cutting,Jinhoon Jeong,Mika A. Sillanpää*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在毫开尔文温度下，用87 mg源质量的微米级相干位移驱动载有1.3 mg检验质量的振动膜，通过微波光力测量实现小尺度引力探测，总质量组合下灵敏度提升两个数量级，kHz以上频段引力灵敏度改善35 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力是已知最弱的相互作用，微观尺度小质量间的引力太弱难以实验探测；广义相对论与量子力学的界面缺乏令人满意的理论统一，而真正探索量子引力需要引力质量处于非经典态，这要求极低温和微小运动范围等极端隔振条件。

Method: 在毫开尔文温度环境中，使87 mg源质量做微米级相干位移，其引力作用于载有1.3 mg检验质量的振荡薄膜运动；采用微波光力（optomechanical）方案测量薄膜振动，该方案是研究机械振子非经典运动态的关键装置。

Result: 在源质量与检验质量总质量组合的指标下，小尺度引力传感较此前工作推进两个数量级；kHz及以上频率的引力灵敏度提升35 dB。

Conclusion: 该装置为研究处于非经典运动态的质量间引力相互作用提供了可行途径，是迈向微观尺度量子引力实验探测的可行方案。

Abstract: Gravity is by far the weakest among all known interactions. While it dominates the large-scale structure of the universe, gravitational interactions between small masses in the microscopic range remain underexplored because they are too weak to be experimentally accessible. The interface between gravity, described by general relativity, and quantum mechanics, which describes the microscopic world, is of particular interest. From a theory standpoint, these two major descriptions of nature have not been combined in a satisfactory manner. Technology is gradually becoming mature enough such that probing gravity at the microscopic scale, where quantum mechanics and gravity potentially meet, is becoming accessible. Explorations towards genuine quantum gravity have to be carried out under conditions where the gravitating masses possess non-classical properties, which necessitates ultimate protection against disturbances, such as ultralow temperatures and a small range of motion. Here, we present an experiment at millikelvin temperatures, where the gravity due to a micron-range coherent displacement of an 87 mg source mass imprints on the motion of an oscillating membrane loaded with a 1.3 mg test mass. Vibrations of the membrane are measured in the microwave optomechanical scheme, which is a key setup for investigating non-classical motional states of mechanical oscillators. When considering the combination of total source and test masses, our setup presents two orders of magnitude progress in sensing gravity at the small scale, as well as a 35 dB improvement of gravitational sensitivity at kHz frequencies or above. Our setup offers a plausible approach for studying gravitational interactions between masses prepared in non-classical motional states.

</details>


### [9] [What are local complementations, really?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08620)
*Kenneth Goodenough*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出图上局部互补（local complementation）可自然地理解为稳定子态（matroid）基的切换（switching），并据此揭示顶点子式与局域Pauli测量统计等结构性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部互补操作在表面上显得奇特，但它是描述图态之间局域Clifford等价的核心工具，已成为图态研究的基础概念。作者希望找到比图论描述更能看清稳定子态结构本质的语言。

Method: 沿Bouchet的思路，将图上的局部互补重新表述为稳定子态的（多拟阵）基的切换（switchings of matroid bases），从而把图论问题转化为拟阵/多拟阵的基切换问题。

Result: 基与切换的视角自然导出顶点子式（vertex-minors）以及对局域Pauli测量统计的描述；同时简化了若干既有关于"图在局部互补下"的证明。

Conclusion: 该工作为多拟阵在量子信息理论中的应用提供了入门性介绍，说明拟阵基语言是研究稳定子态的有力统一框架。

Abstract: A local complementation at a vertex $v$ of a graph $G$ replaces the induced neighborhood of $v$ with its complement. Local complementations are, at face value, peculiar operations. Yet they famously show up when describing the local Clifford equivalence among graph states, and, because of this, have become a fundamental notion in the study of graph states. Following Bouchet, we show here how local complementations on graphs are more naturally thought of in terms of switchings of (matroid) bases of stabilizer states. Bases and switchings reveal structural properties of stabilizer states that are harder to glean from the graph-theoretic description alone, such as vertex-minors and statistics of local Pauli measurements. Bases also simplify prior proofs about graphs up to local complementations, of which we give several examples. Bases are foundational in the study of (multi)matroids; as such this paper serves as a gentle introduction to the applications of multimatroids to quantum information theory.

</details>


### [10] [How Many Shots Does a Quantum Reservoir Need? Measurement Cost and Its Scaling in Buffered Quantum Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08632)
*Raman Khurana*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子储备池计算（QRC）中测量次数是无经典对应物的核心超参数。本文区分"精度预算"N_ε（将储备池特征估计到固定相对精度所需shots）与"收敛预算"N_⋆（使训练后读出逼近其无噪声上限所需shots），并证明二者不一致具有系统性。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QRC中只有线性读出被训练，但储备池状态永远不能直接观测，每个特征都是有限shots下的期望值估计，因此shot预算是首要超参数。已有工作开始研究其影响，但"所需预算应如何定义"以及"不同定义是否一致"仍未解决。

Method: 提出两种预算定义：精度预算N_ε（固定相对精度估计特征所需shots）与收敛预算N_⋆（训练读出逼近自身无噪声天花板所需shots）；并通过命题（Proposition 1，confound）从数学上分析N_⋆与无噪声误差下限的构造性依赖关系。

Result: 命题表明N_⋆按构造依赖于无噪声误差下限，因此误差下限更差的配置反而显得"更便宜"（所需预算更小），造成比较中的系统性偏差而非偶然现象。

Conclusion: 两种预算定义并不一致，且不一致是系统性的。因此配置比较应在固定测量预算下进行，而非按各自达到自身天花板所需的预算来比较。

Abstract: Quantum reservoir computing (QRC) inherits from classical reservoir computing the property that only a linear readout is trained, but unlike a classical reservoir its state is never directly observable: every feature is an expectation value estimated from a finite number of measurement shots. The shot budget is therefore a first-class hyperparameter with no classical analogue. Its effect on QRC has recently begun to be studied; what remains unsettled is how a required budget should be defined, and whether competing definitions agree. This paper argues that they do not, and that the disagreement is systematic rather than incidental.
  This paper distinguishes a \emph{precision budget} $N_\varepsilon$, the shots needed to estimate the reservoir features to a fixed relative accuracy, from a \emph{convergence budget} $N_\star$, the shots needed for the trained readout to approach its own noise-free ceiling. Proposition~\ref{prop:confound} shows that $N_\star$ depends on the noise-free error floor by construction, so that a configuration with a worse floor appears cheaper, and that configurations should therefore be compared at a fixed measurement budget rather than by the budget each needs to reach its own ceiling.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) encodes a combinatorial objective as an Ising Hamiltonian and alternates cost and mixer operations for p rounds. Recursive QAOA (RQAOA) scales further by using the resulting correlations, whether variables tend to agree or disagree, to eliminate variables one at a time. But each inferred sign is accepted irrevocably, so a single early mistake propagates through every later reduction and can discard the global optimum. As elimination proceeds, residuals shrink while effective edge density typically rises, so later decisions face denser Ising problems, a regime that fully connected Sherrington Kirkpatrick (SK) instances stress test directly. Matched simulator and device cells show consistent beam rankings: simulation and hardware agree that beam recovers near-optimal approximation ratio (AR) where standard recursion fails. These gains come from additional residual-circuit evaluations, classical pruning, and exact solution of a small core, rather than from increasing p, beam is a decision-side optimization atop fixed-p QAOA/RQAOA, not a claim that quantum circuits alone scale with qubit count.

</details>

Through the standard propagation rules they raise 1,873 entries of the table. For 190 of the codes, a random Clifford encoder is followed by expurgation: every logical operator of weight below the target distance is enumerated, and a few logical operators are promoted to stabilizers so that each low-weight one anticommutes with at least one of them. The other two are obtained by puncturing a code from the table and repairing it in the same way. All 192 distances are exact: the lower bound is certified by exhaustive meet-in-the-middle enumeration of up to $4\times10^{13}$ Pauli operators, and the upper bound by an explicit logical operator of weight $d$. A first-moment count explains the results. Expurgated random codes have about eight more logical qubits than a typical random code of the same length and distance, so they beat the table wherever its best known codes are no better than random. The largest gains, up to nine logical qubits, are at $d\ge9$ and $n\gtrsim170$, where the table often gives a lower bound without an explicit code. The same count predicts improvements at 619 further pairs $(n,d)$. The search was run by AI agents. The codes, their certificates and an independent checker are public.

</details>

When comparing noiseless, shot-sampled emulation-derived quantum features with a neutral-atom QPU implementation, the downstream hybrid results with compressed hardware features match their emulated counterparts within $0.01$ F$_1$ on all backbones. On a synthetic family of ladder graphs, QGFs distinguish two specific classes, outperforming the strongest classical structural descriptors by a margin exceeding cross-fold variability.

These processes can yield the same Hahn-echo coherence time yet produce different coherence signals under pulse control of the qubit. Existing theoretical approaches either capture bath correlations at a cost that grows combinatorially with spin-bath cluster size or neglect polarization transport altogether. Importantly, we find that whether a rate description suffices is set not by coupling strength alone but by whether a pair can exchange polarization coherently before detuning and damping suppress it.

TL;DR: 本文将 Noether 定理中沿经典路径消失的 Euler-Lagrange 积分项解释为量子涨落，用路径积分算出其表达式，并以谐振子为例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Noether 流推导中出现的、对 Euler-Lagrange 方程积分的那一项在经典路径上为零，因而通常被丢弃；但量子粒子不走经典路径，该项不应为零。作者据此把该项重新解释为 Noether 流的量子涨落，并希望在 Noether 定理与路径积分框架下给出其显式表达式。

Method: 结合 Noether 定理与 Feynman 路径积分：保留传统上被丢弃的 Euler-Lagrange 积分项，将其视为粒子偏离经典路径所导致的量子涨落；对时间平移不变系统求该泛函，取其在偏离经典路径的位移 y 处的值，得到与哈密顿量泛函相关的结果；再以单谐振子为具体模型计算能量的量子涨落。

Abstract: Recent experimental demonstrations of Quantum Key Distribution (QKD) have attracted significant attention for their ability to establish information-theoretically secure encryption keys for next-generation communication systems. Abstract: Krylov subspace methods efficiently approximate extremal eigenstates and eigenvalues of many-body Hamiltonians, but non-orthogonal bases can suffer from ill-conditioning. Abstract: How a quantum resource can be quantified depends on which operations are free. Abstract: Moment-based tomography efficiently reconstructs Gaussian states, but stochastic phase noise generally produces mixed, non-Gaussian outputs. Our trace-distance guarantee separates the intrinsic Gaussian approximation error from statistical errors in estimating the first moments and covariance matrix. Using recent Gaussian trace-distance bounds, we obtain linear dependence of the statistical contribution on moment errors when the covariance inverses remain bounded, improving on the square-root dependence on covariance error in earlier guarantees. A Fock-space truncation procedure provides certified, convergent lower and upper bounds on the intrinsic error. Under an explicit sub-Gaussian condition on the heterodyne outcomes, the sufficient sample count scales inversely with the square of the allowed statistical error remaining after accounting for the approximation error. We verify the statistical condition for stochastic phase rotations and heralded unitary-averaged states with bounded input squeezing. We apply the framework to probabilistic unitary averaging for phase noise reduction, quantifying reconstruction error and accounting for heralding overhead in the total number of estimation attempts.

The shared reservoir enhances the capacity for couplings weaker or stronger than both local dissipation rates, and can increase the extractable work even where it reduces the capacity. At strong drive, with the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction between the charger and the battery, the capacity is bounded by $1/2$. For qubits arranged in a star network with a single central battery qubit, these results remain qualitatively unchanged, while the maximum capacity of a battery charged by $N_A$ weakly driven charger qubits saturates with $N_A$ in the presence of the shared reservoir, approaching its limit as $N_A^{-1}$. Abstract: Symmetry breaking in mixed quantum states exhibits a rich strong-to-weak hierarchy, yet the use of symmetry as an operational probe of dissipative criticality remains largely unexplored. Abstract: Task-oriented quantum sensing aims to extract decision-relevant information directly, without first reconstructing the full signal. We consider both the sensor-ensemble limit and strict single-sensor single-shot readout. Increasing QCS control depth can improve robustness and class-dependent output contrast. Ensemble accuracy can rise rapidly for some waveform pairs, whereas single-shot correctness improves more gradually because one projective outcome must distinguish the classes. Repeated acquisitions improve both routes by averaging independent fluctuations, while disorder-aware training mitigates quasistatic inhomogeneity in the modeled sensor ensemble. QCS can outperform inductive readout under the modeled conditions, for example when coil transfer is weak or receiver noise limits coil performance. However, the simulated QCS protocols at lower effective sensor coupling require long sensing intervals, whereas short-time protocols require a much stronger effective drive at the sensor. Practical advantage therefore depends on jointly attainable source-signal strength, signal-to-sensor transfer, qubit coherence, and readout resources.

The dressings have Clifford level at most one less than the degree of the twist, and for cubic twists the code dimension is a count of solutions of quadratic equations in cup products, which certifies that the code is not a Pauli stabilizer code when it is not a power of two; on tori and slabs the distance is linear in the size. A quarter-turn twist of the 3D fermionic toric code gives codes of linear distance, from $[[768,2,9]]$ up, whose $p+ip$ pump is an explicit finite-time quasi-local evolution that preserves the code space exactly; using the spin-topological response formula of Barkeshli, Hsin and Kobayashi, we identify its logical action as $T$ up to Clifford factors. Abstract: Time crystals are phases of matter that spontaneously break time-translation symmetry. They have been ruled out in the ground and Gibbs states of isolated systems with only short-range interactions, yet they have been proposed and observed experimentally in nonequilibrium settings such as Floquet and open systems. However, existing proposals for time crystals in open quantum systems require an external supply of thermodynamic resources, such as work or energetic coherence. Whether coupling to an environment, without any such resource supply, can induce a time crystal absent in the isolated system remains open. Here, we answer this question systematically. We first construct an explicit model and prove that coupling to an environment can activate a time crystal absent in the isolated system, even without such resource supply. We consider Lindblad master equations with (i) time-independent Hamiltonian and jump operators, (ii) time-translation covariance, and (iii) detailed balance---conditions that hold in standard derivations unless thermodynamic resources are supplied externally. Our model satisfies these conditions, and the activation occurs in a stationary state of both the isolated and open systems. We give several implementations of this dynamics with a zero-temperature Gibbs environment, a time-independent, energy-conserving interaction, and only short-range Hamiltonians, one of which exactly reproduces the time-crystalline periodicity. We then prove a no-go theorem: under (i)--(iii), activation can arise only from an environment-induced Hamiltonian term, not from dissipation. Our results show that time crystals can be activated in open systems without external resource supply and identify the physical requirements on the system and environment for this activation through our general no-go theorem.

Abstract: Free fermions exhibit Eigenstate Typicality (ET) meaning that the reduced density matrix, $ρ$, of a small spatial subsystem of a {\sl single}, typical, excited energy eigenstate is gaussian with a modular hamiltonian, $h \equiv -\log ρ$, asymptotically proportional to the free fermion hamiltonian. Measurements of the single particle distribution of a small subsystem would then exhibit non-thermal deviations from the FD distribution. These features may be traced to a violation of Wick's theorem for the chosen excited state. We develop a perturbation theory for the modular hamiltonian corresponding to a weak mixture of degenerate states and study the modular hamiltonian (exactly) computationally. Finally, we speculate that initial conditions in a realistic fermi system evolving towards equilibrium might be encoded in the interaction terms of the modular hamiltonian and consequent correlations of fermions.

Abstract: When does a quantum particle arrive at a detector? The temporal distributions of itinerant quantum particles have been debated since the birth of quantum mechanics. Here we study the arrival-time problem in periodically monitored spin chains and quantum circuits. The arrival dynamics exhibits two regimes, the quantum Zeno and the low-monitoring regime, separated by an optimal monitoring period set by the single-particle bandwidth. In support of the theory, on superconducting quantum hardware, we measure arrival-time distributions for single and multiple interacting particles in one- and two-dimensional circuits. The measured distributions resolve both regimes and agree closely with quantum-trajectory theory. The crossover persists in interacting many-particle systems: the optimal monitoring point is preserved while many-body interference reshapes the distributions. Zeno suppression likewise persists in two-dimensional geometry. Together, these results show that the two regimes are a generic feature of monitored propagation rather than a single-particle effect. To our knowledge, our work constitutes the first experimental measurement of arrival-time distributions in a monitored quantum transport setting, addressing one of the longest-standing problems in quantum mechanics.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NP问题的经典穷举搜索最坏查询复杂度为O(N)（N=d^n）；量子搜索可降至O(√N)，但常规量子搜索通常假设已给定识别解的oracle，且标准实现只能概率性成功。因此需要解决"如何由经典验证函数构造oracle"以及"如何确定性获得解"两个问题。

Abstract: Problems in the class NP are important in computational science, where a given candidate solution can be verified in polynomial time, while finding a solution may require evaluating the entire search space in the worst case. Classical exhaustive approaches generally solve such problems by evaluating the verifier function over all possible inputs, resulting in a worst-case query complexity of O(N), where N = d^n represents the size of the search space, d is the number of possible values for each variable, and n is the number of variables. Quantum search algorithms can solve unstructured search problems more efficiently by reducing the query complexity to O(sqrt (N)), providing a quadratic speedup over classical exhaustive search. However, conventional quantum search formulations typically assume access to an oracle that identifies valid solutions, while standard implementations generally yield probabilistic success. In this work, we propose a generalised quantum oracle construction method for NP problems, together with a deterministic quantum search framework that identifies satisfying assignments using their corresponding classical verifier functions. The proposed framework retains the quadratic query speedup of quantum search over classical exhaustive search. We further establish that constructed oracle can be implemented using a number of gates polynomial in the size (L) of the binary encoding of verification function, consistent with the polynomial-time evaluation of the corresponding classical verifier, while the total qubit requirement remains O(poly(L)). The construction is demonstrated on multiple N-SAT instances, with circuit-level simulations confirming deterministic identification of satisfying assignments. These results establish the applicability of deterministic quantum search to NP problems while retaining the quadratic query advantage over classical exhaustive search.

</details>

Abstract: We propose a definition of non-equilibrium Gibbsian entropy applicable to general quantum systems, computable from Wigner functions, for both Gaussian and non-Gaussian states. We show that while the Wigner function is a quasi-probability distribution, fundamental results linking observables in repeated observations to the function, such as the law of large numbers, the central limit theorem and, crucially, the relation between the entropy and the cumulant generating function continue to apply. This ideas are the core of the large deviation theory, which allow to formulate a positive-definite definition of entropy based on our ignorance of the underlying state structure after repeated measurements of cumulants. This new definition is a parallel to the Gibbs entropy formulation, but it can also be used in out-of-equilibrium regimes. Some important properties of this entropy are discussed. In addition we derive some information theory quantities such as relative entropy, conditional entropy, mutual information and Rényi entropy. We then compare the results with Wehrl entropy for the 1D harmonic oscillator, for pure and mixed states. We conclude the the work discussing potential applications of this new formalism, ranging from quantum correlations beyond entanglement to thermalization in closed quantum systems.

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一条系统、可扩展的途径来构造驱动系统在关联经典噪声下的时间局域主方程，兼顾非对易时序效应与完全正性，并为非高斯噪声的推广提供了自然路径。

Abstract: We introduce a modular framework for deriving time-local quantum master equations for driven systems subject to temporally correlated classical noise. The construction combines the Donsker-Furutsu-Novikov relation with the Magnus expansion: Functional response closes the noise average, while nested commutators retain the noncommuting chronological action of noise-induced perturbations along the controlled quantum trajectory. For zero-mean Gaussian noise, we derive the resulting Novikov-Magnus quantum master equation through fourth order in the noise strength. The same response hierarchy suggests a natural extension beyond Gaussian noise, with connected higher-order noise correlations weighting higher functional derivatives of the stochastic map. For a driven qubit under Ornstein-Uhlenbeck and band-limited $1/f$ noise, the second- and fourth-order maps remain CPTP throughout the perturbative regime and accurately reproduce stochastic simulations. Their diamond-norm errors are comparable to those of the cumulant-based pseudo-Lindblad master equation, with a modest fourth-order improvement for Ornstein-Uhlenbeck noise.

</details>

Abstract: The quintom model consisting of quintessence and phantom scalar fields with linear coupling to matter is explored and constrained by updated DESI and supernovae type Ia~(SNIa) observational data. The matter interaction is assumed only between phantom and matter and the quintessence self potential is in an exponential form. The quintom evolution shows a class of solutions with phantom crossing scenario consistent with the current DESI data. From low redshift observations, we place constraints on the model with BAO data from DESI and distance measurements from SNIa Pantheon+ samples and SH0Es. For high-redshift constraints, we use CMB priors from Planck and found that the quintom model gives the best-fit Hubble parameter $H_{0}=65.780~(68.736)$~km s$^{-1}$ Mpc$^{-1}$ for DESI+CMB+SNIa~(+SH0ES) respectively. The marginalized statistical constrained values are $H_{0}=66.21\pm 0.84~(68.49^{+0.46}_{-0.40})$~km s$^{-1}$ Mpc$^{-1}$ for DESI+CMB+SNIa~(+SH0ES) respectively. Using the BBN prior for high-redshift constraints, the corresponding best-fit values for DESI+BBN+SNIa~(+SH0ES) constraints are $H_{0}=63.999~(71.962)$~km s$^{-1}$ Mpc$^{-1}$ and the marginalized statistical values for DESI+BBN+SNIa~(+SH0ES) constraints are $H_{0}=61.6^{+6.6}_{-3.0}~(71.86\pm 0.88)$~km s$^{-1}$ Mpc$^{-1}$ respectively. Generally, adding SH0ES significantly raises the best-fit and marginalized value of $H_{0}$ for all datasets. In comparison to $Λ$CDM, the interacting quintom model gives lower values of $σ_{8}=0.74-0.79$ for combination of datasets with CMB prior. Lastly, the fifth-force constraint from local scale solar system Cassini probe is estimated, we find the constraint on the phantom-matter coupling $|δ|<3.3\times 10^{-3}$.

TL;DR: 本文提出 Phentax，即 IMRPhenomTHM 波形模型的 JAX 重实现，为 LISA 全局拟合提供快速、可批量计算且可微的波形计算工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用 JAX 对含高阶模式的可比质量双黑洞时域唯象波形模型 IMRPhenomTHM 进行重实现，得到 Phentax；利用 JAX 的即时编译、向量化与自动微分能力实现高效批量与可微计算。

Abstract: The upcoming Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) is expected to be sensitive to thousands of white dwarf (WD) binaries in our galaxy, extreme mass ratio inspirals (EMRIs) of stellar-mass compact objects into massive black holes, stellar-mass black hole binaries (SOBHBs) in their early inspiral phase, and, crucially, massive black hole binaries (MBHBs) with masses in the range of $10^4$-$10^7 M_\odot$. The presence of such a large number of signals requires joint inference of all parameters across all sources present in the data, an approach referred to as "global fit". This requires evaluating hundreds of thousands of waveform templates, which can be computationally expensive. To address this challenge, we present $\texttt{Phentax}$, a $\texttt{JAX}$ re-implementation of the $\texttt{IMRPhenomTHM}$ waveform model. This is a state-of-the-art time-domain phenomenological waveform model for comparable-mass binary black holes that includes the effects of higher-order modes. Given this, $\texttt{IMRPhenomTHM}$ is one of the models currently used to simulate and analyze MBHB signals in LISA. With $\texttt{Phentax}$, we provide a fast, easy-to-batch, and differentiable implementation of this waveform model to accelerate the development of LISA global fit algorithms.

</details>

Abstract: Radially excited mini-boson stars have zero modes under static axisymmetric perturbations, at which they bifurcate into axisymmetric configurations with chain-like and ring-like matter distributions. We study how self-interactions affect the $\ell=2$ zero modes of spherical boson stars with one radial node, and construct the chains and rings that bifurcate from them. We consider massive and solitonic boson stars, which have a repulsive quartic self-interaction and a Q-ball type potential, respectively. For massive boson stars, the zero mode persists for all values of the quartic coupling we considered. As the coupling grows, it moves onto the candidate stable branch, so that this branch cannot be entirely stable against $\ell=2$ perturbations, even though sufficiently strong self-interactions are known to quench the radial instability of these stars. For solitonic boson stars, the bifurcations depend strongly on the gravitational coupling. As it decreases, a single family of spherical stars can have multiple zero modes, at which chains and rings alternate, and below a critical coupling the zero modes disappear. In flat spacetime, the rings still exist as static ring-like Q-balls, whereas no chains are found, which suggests that chains need gravity to exist.

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) is a space-based gravitational wave detector scheduled for launch in the 2030s by the European Space Agency. Operating in the 0.1 mHz to 0.1 Hz band, LISA will observe a variety of astrophysical sources including but not limited to supermassive black hole mergers, extreme mass ratio inspirals, and white dwarf binaries. LISA will yield new insights, including supermassive black hole formation, new tests of general relativity, population studies of white dwarfs and others. Like all interferometric gravitational wave detectors, LISA data is expected to contain non-astrophysical transients, commonly referred to as glitches. We introduce a two step pipeline that consists of a search algorithm for detecting potential transients in simulated LISA data followed by a machine learning model that classifies the detected transients as gravitational waves or instrumental glitches. This is important for MBHBs with very high masses (around $10^6\ M_{\odot}$) which are transients that could be morphologically similar to LISA glitches in the data. Initial results demonstrate a promising ability to classify detected events with an accuracy close to $98\%$. The pipeline also shows the ability to analyze a month of data in under 2 minutes on a laptop. This shows an eventual utility of our pipeline for enabling accurate rapid alerts from low latency searches. It could also support model selection in global-fit analyses by providing an initial summary of the location and type of individual signals. This paper also discusses challenges encountered during training, including limitations of the current framework.

Keeping the pulse parameters unchanged, we find a larger residual reset error closer to extremality. For comparison, an unconfined scalar field has near-extremal luminosity $P_\infty\propto y^4$, with $y=r_H/|g|-\sqrt2$. Assuming a particular quasistatic energy-loss prescription gives $y\propto t^{-1/2}$; this does not establish the existence of a stable remnant. For this particular reset operation, vanishing Hawking temperature therefore does not force the transferred heat to vanish.

We estimate, assuming an ideal element-to-element spatial distance estimator and a wave of amplitude $h_0 = 10^{-2}$, that at least $N_{\mathrm{total}} \gtrsim \mathcal{O}(10^8)$ elements are needed to overcome the noise introduced by the thickness and detect the curvature. Abstract: We present an alternative formulation of coupled gravitational and electromagnetic perturbations of Kerr--Newman--de Sitter black holes, yielding decoupled radial and angular equations with a well-defined $Λ\rightarrow 0$ limit. We compute the quasinormal mode spectra using spectral and direct integration methods, finding excellent agreement. All of these modes are consistent with the linear stability of the Kerr--Newman--de Sitter black hole. We investigate the photon sphere and near-extremal modes, revealing a non-trivial interplay between rotation and electric charge. We further explore the implications for the strong cosmic censorship conjecture and find that the photon sphere modes predominantly determine its validity within the parameter space considered. While increasing the rotation of the black hole generally favours the strong cosmic censorship conjecture, increasing the electric charge can promote its violation. We also find that the region of parameter space exhibiting a violation of the conjecture is comparatively smaller for gravitational perturbations than for electromagnetic perturbations. These results highlight the important role of rotation, electric charge, and the nature of perturbations in determining the validity of the strong cosmic censorship conjecture in Kerr--Newman--de Sitter spacetimes.

</details>


### [78] [Scalar Black-Holes in Gravity from Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09737)
*David S. Pereira,Udaykrishna Thattarampilly,Vishnu Kakkat*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究 Gravitation from Entropy (GfE) 中静态球对称黑洞，在玻色形式空间场为纯标量的扇区中，证明正则标量毛发的存在性受精确积分恒等式约束：当标量与径向辅助场本征值为正且有效质量平方 m²+ξR 非负时，无穷远处趋于零的正则标量必须恒为零，从而排除 m²≥0 的最小耦合静态标量毛。并借助微扰真空几何的曲率定位非最小耦合可规避符号论证的区域，进一步表明 m²+ξR<0 本身不足以产生正则静态零模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验 GfE 这一熵引力框架下静态球对称黑洞能否携带标量毛，澄清辅助场（Dirac–Kähler）方程对标量自由度的约束，并给出排除标量毛的严格判据及其适用范围。

Method: 从完整辅助场方程出发，将 Dirac–Kähler 物质方程分解为标量方程与两形式相容性条件（径向标量自动满足后者）；对一般反作用静态球对称几何推导精确积分恒等式，据此给出正则标量必为零的符号论证；再计算微扰 GfE 真空几何，用其曲率识别非最小耦合可规避符号论证的区域，并作更精细估计以判断零模分岔。

Result: 得到适用于一般反作用静态球对称背景的精确积分恒等式；证明在采样标量与径向辅助场本征值为正、m²+ξR≥0 时，无穷远趋于零的正则标量必为零，特别排除正则 identity-connected 分支上 m²≥0 的最小耦合静态标量毛；给出微扰 GfE 真空几何并定位非最小耦合的规避区域；进一步表明 m²+ξR<0 并不充分，仍存在一段非最小耦合区间不存在从真空解分岔的支。

Conclusion: GfE 纯标量扇区中，正则静态标量毛发的存在受到严格限制：符号条件成立时无标量毛，非最小耦合即使使有效质量变负也仅在一段有限耦合区间内不足以产生正则静态零模，标量毛的可能仅局限于特定非最小耦合与曲率区域。

Abstract: We investigate static, spherically symmetric black holes in Gravity from Entropy (GfE), focusing on the sector in which the bosonic form-space field is a pure scalar. Starting from the full auxiliary-field equations, we show that the Dirac--Kähler matter equation contains both a scalar equation and a two-form consistency condition. The latter is automatically satisfied by a radial scalar. We derive an exact integral identity, valid for a general backreacted static spherical geometry, and prove that a regular scalar approaching zero at infinity must vanish if the sampled scalar and radial auxiliary-field eigenvalues are positive and the effective mass squared $m^2+ξR$ is nonnegative. In particular, this excludes minimally coupled static scalar hair with $m^2\geq0$ on the regular identity-connected branch. We also present the perturbative GfE vacuum geometry and use its curvature to identify the region in which nonminimal coupling can evade the sign argument. A second, sharper estimate shows that making $m^2+ξR$ negative is not by itself sufficient to produce a regular static zero mode: a finite interval of nonminimal couplings still admits no branch bifurcating from the vacuum solution.

</details>


### [79] [Generalized Schwarzian thermodynamics from gravitational perturbations of near-extremal Kerr](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09757)
*Marcela Cárdenas,Benjamín Cuevas-Tapia,Jessica Trespalacios*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文直接从近极端Kerr近视界几何的四维引力扰动出发，推导出广义Schwarzian热力学，并证明两种精确线性扰动分别在对偶标量JT方程框架下生成Schwarzian模式与静止KdV层级，且在欧几里得区每个模式独立给出近极端熵的奇次幂修正塔。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往近极端黑洞的Schwarzian有效理论多由降维或唯象假设得到，缺乏从完整四维引力扰动出发的严格推导；本文旨在建立四维近NHEK扰动与广义Schwarzian热力学之间的直接联系。

Method: 构造近NHEK背景下两族精确线性扰动（一个非归一化模式，改变渐近边界；一个归一化模式，局域于视界附近），二者均为真空Einstein方程约化为对偶标量JT方程的平滑解且无锥奇异；研究其渐近对称性以得到时间重参数化代数，令规范参数依赖Schwarzian模式，由等距条件选出静止KdV层级，并与扰动系数上的真空Einstein方程约束相互印证；最后在欧几里得区计算Hamilton作用。

Result: 两种扰动均满足JT方程且无锥奇异；渐近对称性生成时间重参数化代数，等距条件选取静止KdV层级，且该层级亦由扰动系数上的Einstein方程独立给出；欧几里得Hamilton作用计算表明每个模式独立生成近极端熵的奇次幂修正塔。

Conclusion: 四维近Kerr扰动本身即蕴含广义Schwarzian热力学结构，KdV层级的双重导出与奇次幂熵修正塔表明该框架是近极端Kerr物理的内在特征，而非降维近似产物。

Abstract: We derive the generalized Schwarzian thermodynamics of near-extremal Kerr directly from four-dimensional gravitational perturbations of the near-horizon geometry. We construct two families of exact linear perturbations of the near-NHEK background: a non-normalizable mode that deforms the asymptotic boundary, and a normalizable mode supported near the horizon. Both perturbations are smooth solutions of the vacuum Einstein equations reduced to the Jackiw--Teitelboim equation for the dual scalar, without conical singularities. We study their asymptotic symmetries and show that they generate an algebra of time reparametrizations; requiring the gauge parameters to depend on the Schwarzian mode, the isometry condition selects the stationary KdV hierarchy. The same hierarchy is independently enforced by the vacuum Einstein equations on the perturbation coefficient. In the Euclidean regime, we evaluate the Hamiltonian action and show that each mode independently generates a tower of odd-power corrections to the near-extremal entropy.

</details>


### [80] [Particle production from noncommutative-inspired Reissner-Nordström black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09859)
*Alberto Maya,Marco Maceda*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 利用无质量Klein-Gordon场与移动反射镜模型，计算由抹平质量与电荷分布的非对易启发式Reissner-Nordström黑洞的粒子产生谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非对易几何（抹平质量/电荷分布）如何修正经典Reissner-Nordström黑洞的霍金辐射谱，此前缺乏对移动反射镜框架下非对易截断轨迹发射特性的定量分析。

Method: 采用无质量Klein-Gordon场与移动反射镜（moving mirror）模型，将非对易启发式RN黑洞的抹平源分布映射为反射镜的截断轨迹，并计算对应的粒子发射谱。

Result: 非对易截断轨迹在高频区压制发射谱，其根源是外部表面引力受到非对易效应修正；该行为与经典RN黑洞谱形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 非对易效应对黑洞外部表面引力的修正导致高频辐射抑制，可作为区分非对易启发式黑洞与经典RN黑洞的可观测特征。

Abstract: By considering a massless Klein-Gordon field and the moving mirror model, we calculate particle production for a noncommutative-inspired Reissner-Nordström black hole sourced by smeared distributions of mass and charge. We show that noncommutative truncated trajectories diminish the emission spectrum at large frequencies, due to noncommutative effects on the external surface gravity of the black hole, in sharp contrast with the spectrum of a classical Reissner-Nordström black hole.

</details>

### [81] [FasterRW: An Efficient Prior-Assisted Random Walk Solver for Thermal Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08490)
*Zixiao Wang,Tianshu Hou,Chenghan Wang,Zhen Zhuang,Leilei Jin,Tsung-Yi Ho,Farzan Farnia,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FasterRW 提出基于延拓值的框架，加速 Feynman–Kac 热学随机游走，在无 Dirichlet 边界场景下用先验校正的有效单点估计与 kriging 多点半样本精化，实现最高 28 倍加速且保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际 IC 热仿真中往往没有 Dirichlet 边界，Feynman–Kac 随机游走为控制截断残差需极长路径，计算代价过高。

Method: 证明截断残差停止时恒等式：截断后的期望尾部等于当前衰减权重乘以停止位置的温度；据此构建先验校正单点估计器，用廉价含噪先验替代未知延拓值，使截断偏差随先验误差而非绝对温度缩放，并给出误差可控的阈值选择准则；对多点查询，将跨查询穿透尾部转为伪样本，并在先验误差坐标下用 kriging 精化观测。

Result: 相比此前最先进方法，在保持精度的前提下实现最高 28 倍加速。

Conclusion: 延拓值视角可有效消除长路径带来的截断瓶颈，为局部温度估计提供误差可控的加速方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Thermal simulation is becoming increasingly critical in modern IC design and manufacturing. Feynman--Kac random walk methods can estimate localized temperatures without solving the global field, but in practical settings without Dirichlet boundaries they require very long paths to control the truncation residual. This paper presents FasterRW, a continuation-value framework for accelerating Feynman--Kac thermal random walks. We prove a stopping-time residual identity showing that the expected tail after truncation equals the current attenuation weight multiplied by the temperature at the stopping location. This identity enables a prior-corrected single-point estimator: a cheap, noisy prior replaces the unknown continuation value, making the truncation bias scale with prior error rather than absolute temperature and yielding an error-controlled threshold-selection rule. For multi-point queries, FasterRW further converts cross-query pass-through tails into pseudo-samples and refines the resulting observations in prior-error coordinates through kriging. Compared with prior state-of-the-art methods, FasterRW achieves up to $28\times$ acceleration while maintaining accuracy. Our code is open-sourced at https://github.com/ShiningSord/FastRW.

</details>


### [82] [Enforcing Symmetries on Pre-trained ML Models for Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08992)
*Wenjie Zhou,Valentino F. Foit,David W. Hogg,Soledad Villar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将事后群平均（测试时增强）从紧群推广到非紧群：用Weyl酉技巧、数据依赖正交投影、frame averaging三种替代方案取代归一化Haar平均，并在单位重标度群与Lorentz群上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事后群平均对有限/紧群等价于Reynolds算子，可在不改变架构、不重训的情况下赋予预测器对称性并具备非增风险保证；但非紧群Haar测度不可归一化，群平均无法直接定义，需寻找替代构造并评估其实际效果。

Method: 先在物理仿真模型上做数值实验展示紧群事后平均的有效性；再针对非紧群提出三种替代：(1) Weyl酉技巧（复化空间并延拓预测器）；(2) 数据依赖正交投影（在不变/等变函数空间上的非平凡优化）；(3) frame averaging（将每点映射到轨道代表元集合）。在单位重标度群和Lorentz群上做理论与实证说明。

Result: 非紧情形下，Weyl技巧与frame averaging相对基线无一致提升，仅在测试数据分布外（OOD）时提升达数量级，Weyl技巧另在低数据情形有效；正交投影在分布内与分布外均可带来提升；直接训练于不变特征的模型匹配甚至优于对无约束模型做投影。

Conclusion: 事后对称化可推广到非紧群，但不能稳定优于直接训练对称模型；实践中直接使用不变特征或训练对称模型更可靠。

Abstract: Post-hoc group averaging (or test-time augmentation) is a simple way to impose symmetry on a trained predictor without changing its architecture or retraining it. For finite and compact groups, averaging over a normalized Haar measure on the group gives the Reynolds operator: a canonical projection onto invariant or equivariant predictors with known non-increasing risk guarantees under certain assumptions. This paper provides numerical experiments on physics emulation models that demonstrate the effectiveness of the approach in this setting. We then extend this framework to non-compact group symmetries. For non-compact groups, the Haar measure cannot be normalized, so group averaging cannot be defined in the same way. We replace it with three alternatives: the Weyl unitary trick (which requires complexifying the space and extending the predictor), a data-dependent orthogonal projection (which involves a non-trivial optimization on the space of invariant or equivariant functions), and a frame averaging approach (which involves mapping each point to a set of orbit representatives). We illustrate the constructions theoretically and empirically on the unit-rescaling group and the Lorentz group. Our results show that in the non-compact case, the Weyl trick and frame averaging do not provide a consistent improvement over the baseline, except where the test data is out-of-distribution (an orders-of-magnitude improvement in this case) and, for the Weyl trick, in low-data regimes. The orthogonal projection can provide improvements in-distribution and out-of-distribution. We also observe that models trained directly on invariant features matched or beat the projection of an unconstrained one. We conclude that post-hoc symmetrization extends to non-compact groups, but it does not consistently outperform training a symmetric model.

</details>


### [83] [Embedding Consistent Recurrent Neural Networks in Finite Element Simulations for Path-Dependent Damage Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08494)
*Muhammed Adil Yatkın,Mihkel Kõrgesaar,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"一致性RNN（ConsRNN）"替代网格模型，并将其以Fortran重写嵌入Abaqus/Explicit的VUMAT中作为逐增量断裂判据；ConsRNN在应变路径细化下收敛、与增量数无关，而SimpleRNN随增量数增加产生漂移。嵌入结构级算例后，其峰值力与峰值位移与既有双参数断裂准则一致，但固定分辨率精度有所下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将训练好的机器学习代理模型嵌入显式非线性有限元求解器时，代理模型的预测必须与求解器的应变增量划分数无关；然而显式代码中增量数难以控制，现有RNN代理（如SimpleRNN）会随增量数增加而漂移，因而无法用于嵌入。

Method: 将训练好的RNN损伤判据用Fortran重新实现，嵌入Abaqus/Explicit的VUMAT用户材料子程序，作为"活"的逐增量断裂判据，使网络状态与求解器同步推进；比较两种架构：SimpleRNN与提出的ConsRNN——后者通过设计转移函数，使应变路径细化时预测收敛而非随增量数漂移。两者均在双线性应变路径上训练，并在不同时间离散化与非线性加载历史下评估；最后在结构尺度上以ConsRNN控制夹持钢板单元删除，并与既有双参数断裂准则对比。

Result: SimpleRNN预测随增量数增加而漂移，不适合嵌入；ConsRNN在路径细化下收敛，但在多线性路径上的固定分辨率精度明显低于SimpleRNN。结构尺度上，嵌入的ConsRNN控制单元删除，达到与双参数断裂准则相同的峰值力与峰值位移；该峰前一致性由构造决定（峰前无单元删除，两者共享相同本构路径），差异仅出现在峰后软化段。

Conclusion: 离散一致性是将循环神经网络代理嵌入显式有限元求解器的决定性属性；本文首次在结构尺度上实现了具有非标准转移函数的自定义循环单元的嵌入。同时指出，ConsRNN以固定分辨率精度为代价换取增量无关性，峰前一致性属于构造必然、并非独立验证，真正的模型差异体现在峰后软化响应上。

Abstract: Capturing sheet-metal localisation physics with a trained surrogate inside a finite element (FEM) solver requires the surrogate to deliver consistent predictions irrespective of the solver's strain-increment count, which is difficult to control in non-linear explicit codes. We address this by re-implementing a trained recurrent neural network (RNN) damage criterion in Fortran and embed it as a live, increment-by-increment fracture criterion inside an Abaqus/Explicit user material subroutine (VUMAT), advancing the network state alongside the solver. Two architectures are compared: a SimpleRNN and the proposed Consistent RNN (ConsRNN), whose transition function is designed so that predictions converge as the strain path is refined rather than drifting with the increment count. Both are trained on bilinear strain paths and evaluated under varying temporal discretisations and nonlinear histories. SimpleRNN predictions drift as the increment count increases, which disqualifies it for embedding; ConsRNN converges under refinement, at a substantial fixed-resolution accuracy cost relative to SimpleRNN on multilinear paths. Deployed at structural scale, the embedded ConsRNN surrogate governs element deletion in a clamped steel plate and reaches the same peak force and peak displacement as an established two-parameter fracture criterion. This pre-peak agreement is expected by construction, since no element is deleted before the peak and the two runs therefore share an identical constitutive path; the responses diverge only on the post-peak softening branch. The results establish discretisation consistency as the decisive property for embedding recurrent surrogates in explicit FEM solvers, and report the first embedding of a customised, non-standard-transition recurrent cell at structural scale.

</details>


### [84] [Rethinking Data Selection as Distribution Matching on Semantic Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08495)
*Peng Sun,Yi Yang,Mingshuo Chen,Chunxiao Li,Yao Zhu,Huawen Shen,Tianfan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QMAC将数据选择建模为分布匹配与质量过滤的联合优化：用多频段经验特征函数匹配（全局语义覆盖+局部扰动），结合任务相关质量分数过滤噪声与低质样本，在4类数据、8个模型、44个基准上优于现有基线并部分超过全量训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令微调依赖海量训练数据，带来巨大计算与存储开销，且数据含冗余、噪声与低质样本。现有选择方法多基于多样性、重要性或二者结合，未在嵌入空间中显式约束所选子集与全量数据的分布一致性。

Method: 提出QMAC（quality aware multiband characteristic function matching），将数据选择表述为分布匹配与质量过滤的联合优化问题：在多个频率尺度上匹配所选子集与全量数据的经验特征函数，以同时刻画全局语义覆盖与局部扰动；并利用任务相关质量分数筛除噪声与低质量样本。

Result: 在4种数据类型、8个目标模型、44个基准上系统评估：在标准图文指令数据上优于现有数据选择基线；在标准文本指令、图文推理、文本推理数据上显著超过随机选择；在多种模型与预算组合下超过全量数据训练。

Conclusion: QMAC通过多频段特征函数匹配显式约束分布一致性并结合质量过滤，是一种有效的指令数据选择方法，可在降低成本的同时达到甚至超越全量训练效果；代码将在论文录用后开源。

Abstract: Instruction tuning of large language models and large vision language models relies on massive training data, which incurs substantial computational and storage costs. Meanwhile, training data often contains redundancy, noise, and low quality samples. Existing data selection methods mostly rely on diversity, importance, or a combination of both, without explicitly constraining the distributional consistency between the selected subset and the full data in the embedding space. We propose QMAC, a quality aware multiband characteristic function matching method that formulates data selection as a joint optimization problem of distribution matching and quality filtering. QMAC matches the empirical characteristic functions of the selected subset and the full data across multiple frequency scales, capturing both global semantic coverage and local perturbations, while using task related quality scores to filter noisy and low quality samples. We systematically evaluate QMAC on 4 data types, 8 target models, and 44 benchmarks. Experimental results show that QMAC outperforms existing data selection baselines on standard image-text instruction data, substantially surpasses random selection on standard text instruction, image-text reasoning, and text reasoning data, and exceeds full data training in multiple model and budget combinations. The code will be made publicly available upon acceptance of the paper.

</details>


### [85] [Generative Reconstruction of Full-Field Sensor States for Safety-Critical Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08499)
*Umme Mahbuba Nabila,Linyu Lin,Majdi I. Radaideh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出稀疏传感器重建框架，针对HTTF热工实验比较六种方法（扩散模型DM、VAE、GAN、两种确定性RNN、岭回归），实验内GNN-GRU VAE最优（RMSE 20.54°C，R²=0.98），跨实验迁移中分段上下文DM最优（RMSE 42.84°C，R²=0.91）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键数字孪生的状态感知依赖完整传感器场，但高温辐射环境下热电偶会漂移、失效或间歇性缺失，造成部分可观测性；需要评估不同方法在缺失传感器重建中的表现，尤其是空间建模与生成式建模的价值及跨实验迁移能力。

Method: 基于俄勒冈州立大学HTTF构建稀疏传感器重建框架，设置实验内与实验间两种协议；实验内（PG-29 DCC）模型结合图神经网络空间编码与GRU时序建模，对比DM、VAE、GAN三种条件生成模型、两种确定性RNN与岭回归；实验间以PG-27 PCC训练、PG-29测试，采用分段上下文条件化。

Result: 实验内GNN-GRU VAE误差最低（MAE 11.73°C、RMSE 20.54°C、R²=0.98）；确定性GNN-GRU与纯GRU的RMSE分别为25.26°C与35.22°C，体现空间建模收益；三种生成模型RMSE均较岭回归降低一半以上；已知传感器由91降至45时DM仍保持R²=0.94；跨实验分段上下文DM最优，RMSE 42.84°C、R²=0.91。

Conclusion: 时空生成式建模对安全关键数字孪生中的缺失传感器重建具有显著价值，图结构空间编码可有效提升精度，扩散模型在传感器稀疏化与跨实验迁移场景下鲁棒性最佳。

Abstract: Accurate reconstruction of incomplete sensor fields is critical for state awareness in safety-critical digital twins (DTs). Thermocouples in high-temperature and radiation environments may drift, fail, or become intermittently unavailable, causing partial observability. This study develops a sparse-sensor reconstruction framework for Oregon State University's High Temperature Test Facility (HTTF) and evaluates six methods under intra- and inter-experiment protocols: three conditional generative models (i.e., a diffusion model [DM], variational autoencoder [VAE], and generative adversarial network [GAN]), two deterministic recurrent neural-network models, and ridge regression. For intra-experiment reconstruction within the PG{-}29 depressurized conduction cooldown (DCC) test, the models combine graph-based spatial encoding with gated recurrent units. The graph neural network (GNN)--gated recurrent unit (GRU) VAE achieves the lowest error, with a mean absolute error (MAE) of $11.73,^\circ$C, a root mean square error (RMSE) of $20.54,^\circ$C, and an $R^2$ of 0.98. The deterministic GNN--GRU and plain GRU obtain RMSE values of $25.26,^\circ$C and $35.22,^\circ$C, respectively, demonstrating the benefit of spatial modeling. All three generative models reduce RMSE by more than half compared with ridge regression. The DM maintains an $R^2$ of 0.94 when known sensors decrease from 91 to 45. For inter-experiment reconstruction, models trained on PG{-}27 pressurized conduction cooldown (PCC) are evaluated on PG{-}29 using section-context conditioning. The section-context DM achieves the best transfer performance, with an RMSE of $42.84,^\circ$C and an $R^2$ of 0.91. These results demonstrate the value of spatiotemporal generative modeling for missing-sensor reconstruction in safety-critical DT applications.

</details>


### [86] [Adaptive Depth Aggregation in Graph Transformers for Long-Range Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08500)
*Yifeng Lu,Zhen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出标准残差流以固定单位系数累积各深度计算，形成"残差接口约束"，在节点间贡献效用异质时产生深度聚合错配；作者将Graph Transformer残差计算形式化为逐节点因果历史，并用Attention Residuals实现节点自适应深度聚合，在GPS/GRIT与ECHO五个任务上均取得更低MAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Graph Transformer主要通过全局或结构感知交互改善跨节点通信，但跨节点通信的增强并不能决定不同深度产生的计算应如何组合。标准残差流使用跨节点共享的固定单位系数累加中间贡献，一旦贡献产生，接口无法按节点显式重新加权。作者假设：当中间贡献的相对效用随节点与结构上下文变化时，这一约束会造成深度维度的聚合错配。

Method: 1) 将Graph Transformer的残差计算形式化为逐节点的因果历史，历史项由图传播（graph propagation）与FFN贡献构成，使深度聚合成为显式的接口选择；2) 在该历史上用Attention Residuals（AttnRes）实现节点自适应聚合，同时保留骨干网络的图传播与辅助状态机制；3) 提出Block AttnRes，通过对连续贡献进行池化，降低长历史的routing开销（相对Full AttnRes）；4) 将该接口实例化到GPS与GRIT两个骨干，并在ECHO全部五个任务上评测；5) 通过routing-only donor-swap干预检验错配假设，并进行消融分析关键设计选择。

Result: 两种变体在每个任务上的MAE均低于各自独立调参的骨干模型；每个任务中至少有一种变体在所有对比方法里取得最低平均MAE。routing-only donor-swap干预结果支持深度聚合错配假设；附加消融实验考察了关键设计选择的影响。

Conclusion: 结果表明，节点自适应的深度聚合可作为跨节点通信的有效补充，共同服务于长程图推理。残差接口是Graph Transformer中一个可独立设计并对性能有实质影响的结构维度。

Abstract: Graph Transformers improve long-range node-to-node communication through global or structure-aware interactions. However, improving cross-node communication does not determine how computations produced at different depths should be combined. Standard residual streams accumulate successive computations with fixed unit coefficients shared across nodes. This fixed aggregation is a residual-interface constraint: once intermediate contributions have been produced, the interface cannot explicitly reweight them by node. We hypothesize that this constraint can create a depth-wise aggregation mismatch when the relative utility of these intermediate contributions varies across nodes and structural contexts. To study this hypothesis, we formalize Graph Transformer residual computation as a causal node-wise history of graph-propagation and feed-forward network (FFN) contributions, making depth aggregation an explicit interface choice. We realize node-adaptive aggregation over this history with Attention Residuals (AttnRes) while preserving the backbone's graph-propagation and auxiliary-state machinery. Block AttnRes reduces the routing cost of long histories relative to Full AttnRes by pooling consecutive contributions. We instantiate the interface in GPS and GRIT and evaluate it on all five ECHO tasks. Both variants achieve lower mean absolute error (MAE) than their independently tuned backbones on every task, and at least one obtains the lowest mean among all compared methods in each task. Routing-only donor-swap interventions support the mismatch hypothesis, while additional ablations examine the key design choices. Together, these results support node-adaptive depth aggregation as a useful complement to cross-node communication in long-range graph reasoning.

</details>


### [87] [Emergent Low-Dimensional Topological Dynamics Dictate Generalization and Escape Mechanics in Billion-Parameter Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08502)
*Dongqing Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出"执行子流形"假说：十亿参数模型的优化轨迹被动态约束在极低维子流形（d_eff≤12）上，据此在正则化Hessian度量下推导基于投影子空间持久同调的拓扑泛化界，并给出严格鞍点指数逃逸的Betti数湮灭机制；进一步以随机Lanczos在活跃Hessian子空间内计算持久同调代理，实现Top-SGD/Top-AdamW包装算法，ImageNet上总墙钟训练时间降低14.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代过参数化网络的泛化能力无法用经典统计学习界解释。作者希望从优化轨迹的几何/拓扑结构出发，给出超越环境维度R^N的严格泛化刻画，并把这一理论落地为可用的优化算法。

Method: (1) 假设轨迹不homogeneous采样R^N，而受限于低维执行子流形M_exec（观测d_eff≤12）；(2) 在正则化Hessian度量g_ij=∇_i∇_jL+εδ_ij下，分析损失下级集上的随机Langevin扩散；(3) 基于投影子空间持久同调推导拓扑泛化界；(4) 将Morse指标跃迁与Betti数湮灭（β_1→0）关联，给出各向异性噪声下严格鞍点指数逃逸的精确机制；(5) 为规避维度灾难，用随机Lanczos迭代严格限制在活跃Hessian子空间内计算投影持久同调代理；(6) 据此设计用活跃曲率签名动态调制学习率的Top-SGD/Top-AdamW包装算法。

Result: 在80+架构（ResNet、ViT、7B LLM）上验证框架；ImageNet基准上总墙钟训练时间降低14.1%，且完全计入计算与内存开销。代码开源。

Conclusion: 优化轨迹的低维拓扑结构可作为泛化理论的紧致基础，并能转化为低开销、可即插即用的优化器改进；持久同调代理在活跃子空间内计算使其具备大规模可行性。

Abstract: The remarkable generalization of modern over-parameterized neural networks defies classical statistical learning bounds. Here we establish that optimization trajectories in billion-parameter models do not sample the ambient dimension $\mathbb{R}^N$ homogeneously; instead, they are dynamically constrained to a low-dimensional execution submanifold $\mathcal{M}_{\text{exec}}$ (with empirically observed effective dimension $d_{\text{eff}} \le 12$). By equipping the parameter space with a regularized Hessian metric $g_{ij} = \nabla_i \nabla_j L + εδ_{ij}$ and analyzing stochastic Langevin diffusion on loss sublevel sets, we derive a rigorous Topological Generalization Bound grounded in projected subspace persistent homology. Furthermore, by linking Morse index transitions to Betti number annihilation ($β_1 \to 0$), we formulate an exact mathematical mechanism for exponential escape from strict saddles under anisotropic noise. We validate our framework across 80+ architectures (ResNets, Vision Transformers, and 7B LLMs). To bridge theoretical topology with computational feasibility, we resolve the curse of dimensionality by computing projected persistent homology proxies via randomized Lanczos iterations restricted strictly within active Hessian subspaces. Building upon this mathematical foundation, we introduce Topological-Adaptive Optimization (Top-SGD and Top-AdamW)---a family of wrapper algorithms that dynamically modulate learning rates using active curvature signatures. Extensive benchmarks demonstrate a 14.1\% reduction in total wall-clock training time on ImageNet with all computational and memory overheads fully accounted for. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/imslab-research/Topological-SGD.

</details>


### [88] [Does Country Matter? Evaluating Geographic Sensitivity of Large Language Models in Zero-Shot Chronic Kidney Disease Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08507)
*Sirajam Munira,Muhammad Ashad Kabir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在保持临床输入完全不变的前提下，仅改变提示词中的国家地理属性（25个全球语境），评估Qwen3 8B与Mistral 7B在孟加拉国社区CKD筛查数据（284人，112例CKD/172例非CKD，取两模型基线均正确的156人子集）上的预测稳定性。结果显示：非CKD预测几乎不受地理语境影响，而CKD阳性预测对地理token明显敏感（Qwen3由基线65降至最低50），且国家语境的分组模式不遵循流行病学或医疗体系的相似性，而更可能反映模型内部国家token的表征结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM具备较强零样本推理能力，但对提示语境的敏感性引发部署可靠性担忧。作者关注一个具体而关键的问题：在患者特征完全不变时，提示中嵌入的地理语境本身是否会影响基于LLM的慢性肾脏病（CKD）筛查判断；若成立，则意味着模型可能将地理token当作非临床信号使用，从而在多地区医疗决策支持场景中造成系统性偏差。

Method: 采用孟加拉国社区CKD数据集（284名参与者，112例CKD、172例非CKD）。实验设计为受控消融：固定全部临床输入，仅在提示中系统替换25个国家语境属性。评估两个开放权重模型Qwen3 8B与Mistral 7B；为确保可比性，实验限定在两模型基线预测均正确的156名参与者构成的"common-correct"子集上，比较不同地理语境下阳性/阴性预测数量的变化，并考察国家语境之间的分组结构是否与已知流行病学或医疗体系相似性一致。

Result: 非CKD预测在地理语境间高度稳定，两模型在几乎所有设置下均保持一致的阴性判断。相反，CKD阳性预测敏感度显著更高：Qwen3的CKD阳性预测数从基线65下降至最低50；Mistral的波动幅度较小但不可忽略。此外，国家语境未按已知流行病学或医疗体系相似性聚类，其分组结构似乎反映各模型内部对国家token的表征方式。

Conclusion: 地理token能够在患者输入完全相同的情况下系统性改变临床筛查结果，说明模型对提示语境存在非预期的、与临床无关的敏感性，且这种效应具有方向不对称性（更易削弱阳性识别）。这提示在多样化医疗环境中部署LLM决策支持时必须重视语境鲁棒性评估与校准，单纯在单一地理语境下验证性能不足以证明其可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show strong zero-shot reasoning capabilities, yet their sensitivity to prompt context raises questions about reliability in deployment. This study investigates whether geographic context embedded in prompts influences LLM-based chronic kidney disease (CKD) screening, independent of any change in patient features. Using a community-based CKD dataset from Bangladesh (284 participants with 112 CKD and 172 non-CKD), we systematically varied the country attribute across 25 global contexts while holding all clinical inputs constant, and evaluated two open-weight LLMs (Qwen3 8B and Mistral 7B). Experiments were conducted on a common-correct subset of 156 participants for whom both models produced correct baseline predictions. Results show that non-CKD predictions were highly stable across geographic contexts, with both models maintaining consistent non-CKD predictions in nearly all settings. In contrast, CKD-positive predictions were considerably more sensitive. For Qwen3, CKD prediction dropped from a baseline of 65 to as low as 50 across country contexts, while Mistral showed smaller but non-negligible variation. Country contexts did not group according to known epidemiological or healthcare similarities; instead, groupings appear to reflect how country tokens are internally represented within each model. These findings demonstrate that geographic tokens can systematically shift clinical screening outcomes despite identical patient inputs, with implications for the deployment of LLM-based decision support in diverse healthcare settings.

</details>


### [89] [Interpretable Multifidelity Learning with Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks for Spatiotemporal Nuclear Reactor Flow Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08512)
*Meredith Eaheart,Majdi I. Radaideh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文面向压水堆堆芯193个燃料组件质量流率的时空预测，比较黑箱多保真前馈神经网络（MFNN）与可解释多保真Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（MFKAN），并引入TabDDPM合成数据缓解CFD瞬态稀缺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真CFD瞬态仿真成本极高，且每个保真度仅有单个可用瞬态，数据稀缺；同时需要在保持精度的前提下降低计算成本，并探索预测精度是否受保真度间相关性本质限制，以及能否获得可解释的符号化模型。

Method: 构建多保真代理模型，将廉价低保真数据与有限高保真数据结合；对比两类架构：黑箱MFNN与基于样条（spline）表示、可输出闭式符号方程的MFKAN；用TabDDPM生成合成数据增广跨保真度预测；在组件层级分析预测相关性与真实跨保真度相关性的关系，并进行自评估与真正跨保真度预测的对比。

Result: 堆芯整体跨保真度相关性弱，属困难多保真问题，两架构绝对精度均不高；单步前向预测中MFKAN与MFNN性能相当，且额外给出MFNN无法提供的闭式符号方程。合成数据增强下，KAN与FNN自评估精度相当（Pearson约0.73–0.81），但真正跨保真度预测均很差（0.03–0.06）。组件级分析显示各组件预测相关系数与其真实跨保真度相关系数高度一致（KAN r=0.840–0.896，FNN r=0.804–0.903）。

Conclusion: 预测精度本质上受限于保真度层级之间既有的相关性水平；KAN类多保真建模在所有设置下精度与MFNN相当，同时提供精度达到或超过样条表示的可解释符号方程，是兼顾可解释性与精度的可行替代方案。

Abstract: High-fidelity computational fluid dynamics (CFD) simulations of nuclear reactor systems are computationally expensive, motivating machine learning methods that reduce cost while preserving accuracy. Multifidelity surrogate modeling addresses this by combining inexpensive low-fidelity data with limited high-fidelity data. This work investigates whether the high-fidelity data required to predict the spatiotemporal mass flow rate across all 193 fuel assemblies of a pressurized water reactor (PWR) core can be reduced, comparing black-box multifidelity feedforward neural networks (MFNNs) with interpretable multifidelity Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (MFKANs). Cross-fidelity correlation across the core is weak, making this a challenging multifidelity problem, and neither architecture predicts the transient particularly well in absolute terms. MFKAN nonetheless performs comparably to MFNN for one-time-step-forward prediction while also providing closed-form symbolic equations unavailable with MFNN. To address the scarcity of CFD transients, with only one available per fidelity level, TabDDPM-generated synthetic data was used to augment cross-fidelity forecasting. KAN and FNN achieve comparable self-evaluation accuracy (Pearson correlation of roughly 0.73--0.81), but both perform poorly at true cross-fidelity forecasting (correlations of 0.03--0.06). Assembly-level analysis shows that each assembly's forecast correlation tracks its real cross-fidelity correlation for both architectures ($r=0.840$--$0.896$ for KAN, $r=0.804$--$0.903$ for FNN), confirming that forecasting accuracy is fundamentally limited by how correlated the fidelity levels already are. Overall, KAN-based multifidelity modeling achieves accuracy consistently comparable to MFNN across all settings, while additionally providing symbolic equations that match or exceed the spline representation's accuracy.

</details>


### [90] [What Are LLM Agents Actually Learning on Low-Budget Molecular Optimization Benchmarks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08522)
*Jiayu Qin,Zhengquan Luo,Changyou Chen,Zhiqiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLM智能体在低预算分子优化上的大幅提升主要来自可检索的任务信息（任务名、参考化合物）与宽松的oracle景观，而非在线优化能力：隐藏任务信息后GPT-4o mini的sum AUC从14.44降至4.85，而仅靠确定性检索、不含LLM的基线即可达12.16（为完整智能体的84%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期文献声称LLM智能体在低预算分子优化（如PMO，50次oracle调用）上取得巨大收益，但这些收益可能被任务信息泄漏和宽松的oracle设计所混淆。作者希望拆解并量化各因素的贡献。

Method: 在PMO基准上进行消融实验：(1) 隐藏任务名称与精选参考化合物的"信息遮蔽"条件；(2) 身份盲化条件，仅告知oracle形式（如"与隐藏参考分子的相似度"）；(3) 无LLM的确定性检索基线；并开展23任务2×2完整消融与8任务三随机种子复现。另在AutoDock Vina复合oracle的蛋白靶向基准上，以单编辑随机游走作为下界基线，并设置无检索的种群智能体与REINVENT在匹配50次调用预算下对比作为阳性对照。

Result: PMO 50次调用下，GPT-4o mini智能体sum AUC由14.44降至遮蔽后的4.85；身份盲化条件为5.39；纯检索无LLM基线达12.16（84%）。蛋白靶向基准上，单编辑随机游走三种子得到复合分0.636±0.008、对接改善-1.11±0.04 kcal/mol，构成强下界。阳性对照中，无检索种群智能体在50次调用AUC预算下超过REINVENT，100次调用时取得最佳结果。

Conclusion: 现有LLM智能体的分子优化增益在很大程度上可由任务信息检索和宽容的oracle景观解释，而非在线优化本身。据此建议两项低成本合理性检查：采用"检索安全"的PMO提示设计，以及在基于对接的优化中引入随机游走下界。

Abstract: Recent work reports large gains for LLM agents on low-budget molecular optimization. We measure the contributions of online optimization, retrievable task information, and permissive oracle landscapes. On PMO at 50 oracle calls, a GPT-4o mini agent's sum AUC falls from 14.44 to 4.85 after task names and curated reference compounds are hidden. An identity-blinded condition that states only the oracle form (e.g., similarity to a hidden reference) scores 5.39, while a deterministic retrieval-only baseline with no LLM reaches 12.16 (84% of the full agent). A full 23-task 2x2 ablation and a three-seed replication on eight tasks support this interpretation. On a protein-targeted benchmark with an AutoDock Vina composite oracle, a single-edit random walk is a strong lower bound; across three seeds it obtains composite 0.636 +/- 0.008 and docking improvement -1.11 +/- 0.04 kcal/mol. As a positive control, a retrieval-free population agent exceeds REINVENT at a matched 50-call AUC budget, with its strongest result at 100 calls. These results motivate two inexpensive sanity checks: retrieval-safe PMO prompts and a random-walk floor for docking-based optimization.

</details>


### [91] [Evolutionary Adaptive Metric Learning: A Multi-Objective Framework for Learning Local Distance Functions in Nearest Neighbor Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08549)
*Isaac Touza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EAML框架，通过低维参数化学习实例相关的正定度量，并给出理论保证与实验评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定距离与全局学习度量在整个输入空间施加同一几何结构，而相关特征方向可能随区域变化，因此需要实例依赖的局部度量。

Method: EAML将局部度量分解为：由锚点间softmax插值生成的输入依赖对角缩放，加上全局低秩相关分量；用实数编码精英遗传算法优化由留一预测误差、鲁棒性、度量平滑性与正则化组成的标量目标；并给出正定性、度量等价性、最优解存在性与Lipschitz连续性等理论分析，以及预测稳定性、泛化界与进化收敛性研究。

Result: 在具有区域相关特征差异的合成数据上能恢复局部度量变化并优于全局度量；在13个公开数据集上采用嵌套5折交叉验证显示，EAML仅在部分数据集上有提升，整体未显著优于全局度量基线；在冗余特征与测试时噪声下全局对角度量可能更优。

Conclusion: 明确了进化式局部度量学习有益的适用条件，同时揭示了其实际局限性。

Abstract: Nearest neighbour classifiers inherit the geometry of their distance function. Fixed distances and globally learned metrics apply the same geometry throughout the input space, although relevant feature directions may vary across regions. We propose EAML (Evolutionary Adaptive Metric Learning), a framework that learns instance-dependent positive-definite metrics through a low-dimensional parameterization. Each local metric combines an input-dependent diagonal scaling, generated by softmax interpolation between anchor points, with a global low-rank correlation component. A real-coded elitist genetic algorithm optimizes the parameters using a scalar objective combining leave-one-out prediction error, robustness, metric smoothness, and regularization. We establish positive definiteness, metric equivalence, existence of an optimal solution, and Lipschitz continuity of the metric field. We further investigate prediction stability, generalization bounds, and evolutionary convergence under explicit assumptions. Experiments on synthetic data with region-dependent feature relevance show that EAML can recover local metric variations and outperform global metrics. Evaluation on thirteen public datasets using nested 5-fold cross-validation reveals a more nuanced picture: EAML improves performance on selected datasets but does not significantly outperform global metric baselines overall. Additional experiments show that global diagonal metrics can be preferable under nuisance features and test-time noise. These findings identify the conditions under which evolutionary local metric learning is beneficial and highlight its practical limitations.

</details>


### [92] [How Small Can We Go? Calibrating Activation-Cache Compression for SAE Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08551)
*Abraham Yeung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究激活缓存压缩对稀疏自编码器(SAE)特征的影响，提出通过"校准"方法（同字节重训为上限、重设随机种子为下限）来衡量各压缩编解码器的真实代价，发现简单int4量化在多数站点比重新播种丢失更多特征，而通道标准化可使int4在同文件大小下回到下限之上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练SAE需要海量模型激活缓存，压缩不可避免，但压缩对SAE特征的影响缺乏可靠的测量基准。论文动机是：现有指标（如冻结SAE相关性、L2距离）给出的结论不一致，保留特征比例在不同设置下没有清晰尺度，因此需要一个可校准的正确度量。

Method: 设计两种测试：(1) frozen-SAE测试——将压缩缓存送入原fp32训练的SAE，观察特征激活保持不变的比例；(2) retraining测试——在压缩缓存上重训SAE，统计原特征存留比例。通过校准建立基准：以相同字节重训作为特征保留的上限(ceiling)，以重新随机种子作为下限(floor)，再针对各站点的下限衡量每个压缩codec的效果。在GPT-2 small与Pythia-1.4B上使用TopK SAE，在research-scale预算下比较fp16、fp32最小步长扰动、重新播种、per-token int4、PCA、随机投影、bf16前向、旋转8-bit存储等方案。

Result: retraining测试中保留特征比例缺乏清晰尺度：GPT-2缓存上fp16舍入保留60.6%特征，而仅改动百万分之一的值（fp32最小步长，小八个数量级）只保留70.5%，重新播种则保留23.0%。frozen-SAE测试中相关性随codec精度提高而增大，但plain L2距离给出几乎相同的排序；同一frozen读数在不同站点对应不同的保留比例。校准后：naïve per-token int4在所有测试站点丢失特征多于重新播种，PCA与随机投影在GPT-2上同样如此；通道标准化后int4在同原始文件大小下保留特征回到下限之上；在最小预算下，bf16前向比旋转8-bit存储略多丢失特征。

Conclusion: 压缩对SAE特征的损伤应以站点特定、经过校准（ceiling/floor）的保留特征比例来衡量，而非绝对比例或简单相关性。该结论表明朴素量化与降维可能破坏特征，而逐通道标准化等简单处理可显著改善；校准一个站点需四次训练和两次评估，成本可控。

Abstract: To train a sparse autoencoder (SAE), you need a massive cache of model activations.
