Existing approaches primarily improve LLM-based problem solving through parameter optimization or feedback-driven refinement of prompts, programs, and solution trajectories, leaving acquired expertise either embedded in model weights or scattered across iterative problem-solving processes. As a result,knowledge is difficult to systematically inspect, evaluate, and refine. We propose KnowEvo, a framework that treats externalized knowledge itself as the optimization target. Our key insight is that scientific knowledge is inherently modular: multiple interacting mechanisms jointly determine the design outcomes. This makes knowledge evolution fundamentally challenging, as improvements cannot be attributed to individual components in isolation. To address this, KnowEvo represents knowledge as executable expertise blocks and organizes their refinements into strategy mutation trees, enabling structured exploration over a combinatorial knowledge space. The framework combines a Thompson-style forward allocation strategy that adaptively distributes evaluation budgets across strategy variants with a backward revision process, in which a code agent operates in an isolated sandbox to translate rollout evidence into localized program updates. This design enables persistent accumulation, evaluation, and refinement of mechanistic expertise across iterative experimentation. We evaluate KnowEvo on protein optimization tasks including stability and solubility. Results show that knowledge evolution consistently improves over initial strategy blocks and outperforms monolithic program evolution and prior LLM-guided evolution baselines across metrics such as pass@k, MRR, MAP,and NDCG@5. Code is available at https://github.com/recynie/KnowEvo.

</details>


### [128] [Knowledge Distillation from Neural Networks to Differentiable Logic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09024)
*Hee Yun Suh,Niraj K. Jha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将知识蒸馏引入可微分逻辑网络（DLN），从训练好的MLP教师向DLN学生蒸馏，在不改变架构与推理流程的前提下显著提升其分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DLN兼具可解释性、边友好性与极低推理成本，可与MLP相当的精度，但其精度受困于梯度消失、可扩展性不足和生态缺失，在部分数据集上仍落后于常规神经网络。作者希望在不牺牲DLN固有优势的情况下缩小这一差距。

Method: 提出面向DLN的知识蒸馏框架：以训练好的MLP为教师，将蒸馏目标嵌入DLN原有的两阶段训练循环（神经元函数学习阶段与连接学习阶段），训练结束后丢弃教师。研究四种变体：(1) 联合超参数搜索，同时优化蒸馏权重、温度与架构；(2) 固定架构，对蒸馏权重与温度做网格搜索；(3) 仅在神经元函数阶段蒸馏的单阶段变体；(4) 仅在连接阶段蒸馏的单阶段变体。

Result: 在20个公开表格分类任务上，蒸馏在不显著增加门级运算数量与模型规模的前提下提升了平衡类别准确率，最高提升约15%，部分情况下甚至超过教师模型；提升幅度因数据集而异。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏可作为一种低成本的增强手段有效弥补DLN与MLP之间的精度差距，且蒸馏后的模型仍保留传统训练DLN的可解释性、稀疏性与推理成本，教师不参与推理。

Abstract: Differentiable logic networks (DLNs) are interpretable, edge-friendly classifiers that learn both their logic operators and their connections through gradient descent, achieving accuracy competitive with multi-layer perceptrons at orders-of-magnitude lower inference cost. Their accuracy, however, is bounded by the problems of vanishing gradients, scalability, and lack of an ecosystem, leaving a gap relative to conventional neural networks on several datasets. We introduce knowledge distillation for DLNs to narrow this gap. We distill from a trained multi-layer perceptron teacher into a DLN student, integrating the distillation objective into a two-phase training loop without altering the architecture or the inference procedure. The teacher is discarded after training. Hence, the distilled model retains the interpretability, sparsity, and inference cost of a conventionally trained DLN. We investigate this approach through four variants: a joint hyperparameter search that optimizes the distillation weight and temperature alongside the architecture, a grid search over these two hyperparameters with the architecture fixed, and two single-phase variants that employ distillation during only the neuron-function or only the connection phase. On 20 public tabular classification tasks, distillation improves balanced-class accuracy at negligible cost to gate-level operation counts and model size, though the benefit varies by dataset. Performance improvements of up to 15% are observed, in some cases surpassing the teacher itself.

</details>


### [129] [Function-Space Certification for Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09038)
*Ryan Hsiang,Robert Joseph George,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出函数空间认证框架，将IBP与CROWN推广到神经算子，在整个连续域上给出严格界限，并可扩展到高阶导数以认证PDE残差与解误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子学习无穷维函数空间之间的PDE解算子，其可靠性需要在全定义域上、跨分辨率成立；但现有神经网络验证方法面向向量到向量映射，只能在固定网格分辨率上认证，无法保证整个连续域或其他分辨率下的输出。

Method: 在函数空间中形式化区间界传播（IBP）与CROWN，借助严格数值积分逐层传播上下界函数；并将界传播推广到高阶导数，用于认证PDE残差与解误差界。

Result: 在多种神经算子架构与科学问题上实验，包括用于全球天气预报的5.725亿参数球面傅里叶神经算子，方法能高效给出紧致且覆盖全域的保证。

Conclusion: 该框架填补了神经算子缺乏全域、分辨率无关认证的空白，为可信算子学习奠定基础。

Abstract: Certifying the robustness and physical consistency of scientific machine-learning models ensures that their predictions remain reliable beyond the test set. For neural operators, which learn PDE solution operators by mapping infinite-dimensional function spaces, certification means establishing rigorous, domain-wide guarantees for their output functions. However, standard neural network (NN) verification methods are fundamentally designed for vector-to-vector mappings, and they can only certify neural operators at fixed grid resolutions and provide no guarantees over the entire domain or across another resolution. We close this gap by introducing a function-space certification framework that verifies neural operators across the entire continuous domain. We formulate interval bound propagation (IBP) and CROWN in function space, propagating upper and lower bound functions through each layer using rigorous numerical integration. In addition, we extend bound propagation to higher-order derivatives to certify PDE residuals and solution error bounds. Experiments across several neural operator architectures and scientific problems, including the 572.5-million-parameter spherical Fourier neural operator for global weather forecasting, show that our method produces tight, domain-wide guarantees efficiently, providing a foundation for trustworthy operator learning.

</details>


### [130] [When Can You Trust Neural Critics? Auditing Mutual Information Estimates of Higher-Order Epistasis in Protein Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09046)
*Maryam Rahimimovassagh,Waldemar Karwowski,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文对先前基于变分互信息估计研究 ESM-2 中 GB1 四站点上位性次序依赖的结论进行审计，发现即便使用稳定化估计器，在该维度和样本量下盲搜索互信息估计仍不足以支撑"次序分辨"的依赖声称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互信息估计被广泛用于探测神经表征（含蛋白质语言模型）所编码的信息，但变分 MI 估计器的方差至少随真实 MI 指数增长，这使既有关于"次序分辨上位性"的结论可靠性存疑，需要系统性审计。

Method: 重跑原 ESM-2/GB1 四站点实验以检验跨种子差异与评估配对方案的影响；在原维度上比较三类替代估计器族；在两个其他蛋白质语言模型的表征维度上构造已知 MI 的高斯信道做合成校准；并设置给定真实信号方向、降至一维的"特权信息"对照 critic。

Result: 重跑差异无系统偏差，量级与常规跨种子重训练波动相当，但评估配对方案会显著改变得分；原维度下三类估计器族中两类可跨种子稳定、一类不能；合成校准中三类族在 5,120 与 4,096 维下均显著低估真实信号（尽管信号排序正确、种子稳定）；特权对照 critic 在增益 1.5 时分别恢复 93.3% 与 98.7% 的解析 MI。

Conclusion: 信号在拥有特权信息时可被恢复，但盲估计失败的成因未获识别；作者将检查打包为可复用诊断协议，并断言在当前维度与样本量下，即使稳定化的变分 MI 估计器也不足以支持盲搜索下的次序分辨依赖结论。

Abstract: Mutual information (MI) estimation is used to probe what neural representations encode, including in protein language models, but variational MI estimators have variance that scales at least exponentially in the true MI. We audit a prior neural-MI study of order-resolved epistasis in ESM-2 on the four-site GB1 landscape. Run-to-run differences show no systematic bias and a magnitude comparable to ordinary cross-seed retraining variability, and the evaluation-pairing scheme shifts scores substantially. At the original dimensionality, two of three alternative estimator families stabilize the estimate across seeds and one does not. In a synthetic calibration at the representation dimensionalities of two other protein language models (a Gaussian channel with known MI; no model representations), all three families recover materially less signal than required, despite correct signal ordering and stable seeds. A control critic given the true signal direction, which also reduces its input to one dimension, recovers 93.3% and 98.7% of the analytic MI at gain 1.5 (at 5,120 and 4,096 dimensions), so the signal is recoverable with privileged information, but the control does not identify why blind estimation fails. We package the checks into a reusable diagnostic protocol and conclude that even a stabilized estimator is not currently sufficient for order-resolved dependence claims made under blind search at this dimensionality and sample size.

</details>


### [131] [LeanDojo-v2: A Comprehensive Library for AI-Assisted Theorem Proving in Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09048)
*Ryan Hsiang,William Adkisson,Robert Joseph George,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 LeanDojo-v2 端到端框架，将仓库数据抽取、LLM 微调、证明搜索、AI 自动补全与 LLM 辅助定理证明整合进单一软件库，并配套 Lean4Code 轻量 IDE，降低数学家在领域专用 Lean 代码库上微调模型并进行实验的门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化定理证明（Lean）机器学习近期进展迅速，但流程割裂：数据抽取、模型微调、证明搜索等环节工具分散，需复杂环境配置，数学家与研究者难以在领域专用代码库上快速实验。

Method: 构建统一软件库 LeanDojo-v2，集成仓库数据抽取、LLM 微调、证明搜索、AI 自动补全、LLM 辅助定理证明五大功能；并提供 Lean4Code 轻量 IDE，以面向数学家的简洁 UI 封装上述能力。

Result: 摘要未给出定量实验结果，主要交付为端到端框架与配套 IDE 工具（系统/工具类工作）。

Conclusion: LeanDojo-v2 与 Lean4Code 使数学家无需复杂配置即可在领域专用 Lean 代码库上微调语言模型并开展 LLM 辅助定理证明实验，推动形式化数学与 LLM 的易用化整合。

Abstract: Building on recent progress in machine learning for formal theorem proving in Lean, we introduce LeanDojo-v2: an end-to-end framework that unifies repository data extraction, LLM fine-tuning, proof search, AI autocompletion, and LLM-assisted theorem proving in a single software library. In addition, we provide Lean4Code, a lightweight IDE that integrates the various features of LeanDojo-v2 through a simple, mathematician-friendly UI. These tools will enable mathematicians and researchers to easily fine-tune language models on domain-specific Lean codebases and experiment with LLM-assisted theorem proving without a complex setup.

</details>


### [132] [StreamTab: Training-Free Temporal Link Prediction with Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09052)
*Mingqi Yang,Renqi Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StreamTab 是一种免训练框架，基于仅在合成表格上预训练的冻结表格基础模型做时序链接预测，核心设计为“regime conditioning”（按候选采样方式采样负例）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序图神经网络需逐图单独训练，且模型如何利用交互历史的信号取决于负候选的构成：当负样本也发生过交互时，单纯“是否交互过”不足以为据，需要其他信号。

Method: 用候选对的过去交互统计量作为特征描述；向冻结的表格基础模型展示近期观测交互与采样负对作为带标签示例，由模型对新候选打分。核心的 regime conditioning 按候选的采样分布采样负例，使示例体现模型需做出的区分；新交互到达时只更新统计量与示例、模型参数保持不变（training-free）。

Result: 在13个数据集、21个时序图基线上：historical sampling 下12个数据集AP最高，inductive sampling 下11个最高，random sampling 下7个数据集位列第一或与最佳相差1分以内；同特征同模型下，regime conditioning 相比随机负例在中位数上提升12.6个AP点（historical sampling）。

Conclusion: 冻结的合成表格基础模型经统计特征+上下文示例即可强于专门训练的时序图网络；负例采样机制须与评估候选分布对齐，这是决定性能的关键设计。

Abstract: Temporal link prediction uses past interactions to predict whether two nodes will interact in the future. Temporal graph neural networks achieve strong results on this task, but they are trained separately for each graph. How a model should use interaction history also depends on the candidates it is compared with. A past interaction can distinguish a recurring link from a random pair that has never interacted, but when the negative candidates have also interacted before, other signals are needed. We propose StreamTab, a training-free framework built on a frozen tabular foundation model pretrained only on synthetic tables. StreamTab describes each candidate pair by statistics of its past interactions and shows the model recent observed interactions and sampled negative pairs as labeled examples, from which the model scores new candidates. Its central design, regime conditioning, samples the negative examples according to how the candidates are sampled, so the examples show the distinction the model must make. As new interactions arrive, StreamTab updates the statistics and refreshes the examples while the model parameters stay fixed. On thirteen datasets against 21 temporal graph baselines, StreamTab achieves the highest average precision on twelve datasets under historical sampling and eleven under inductive sampling, and ranks first or stays within one point of the best on seven datasets under random sampling. With the same features and model, regime conditioning raises average precision over examples with random negatives by a median of 12.6 points under historical sampling.

</details>


### [133] [WearableBridge: Task-Guided Latent Transport for Free-Living Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09067)
*Jingjing Zou,Yixin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WearableBridge 用少量参考数据（大腿同步记录或实验室腕部数据）训练一个任务引导的"桥"映射，将腕部特征搬运到参考编码器-分类器所认定的活动区域，从而在不改动参考模型的前提下显著提升仅戴腕部设备者的活动识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腕戴加速度计可大规模做活动监测，但手臂的附带运动、设备朝向变化和情境漂移会模糊区分活动的信号。校准研究可提供高质量参考（如大腿同步记录、实验室监督式腕部采集），但成本高、受试者少、部署时难以获得。核心问题是：如何用有限的参考数据，提升只佩戴腕部设备用户的识别效果。

Method: 提出 WearableBridge：固定参考编码器与分类器，学习一个任务引导的传输映射（桥），把每个腕部特征送入分类器关联其真实活动类别的区域。目的地由参考特征或分类器预测给出，并用分类损失惩罚落入其他活动区域的腕部特征。实例化三种桥：Mapper（回归到参考特征）、Distillation（匹配分类器类别概率）、MeanFlow（学习由腕部指向参考特征的一步流）。

Result: 在 40 名留出的 PAAWS 自由生活受试者上，三种桥在每次训练运行中均提升 macro-F1；最强的 MeanFlow 将平均 macro-F1 从 35.1% 提升至 56.0%（Thigh2Wrist，大腿参考），从 54.0% 提升至 63.5%（Lab2Freeliving，群体匹配的实验室腕部参考）。仅用 PAAWS 拟合时，MeanFlow 在 CAPTURE-24 队列上也将 macro-F1 从 42.8% 提升至 54.7%。增益在 selfBACK 上复现，跨四种参考编码器成立，并随时序上下文增长。

Conclusion: 研究可为校准子集采集参考记录，仅训练 0.13–2.44 百万参数的桥（参考编码器与分类器保持不变），即可提升其余所有仅腕戴用户的活动识别。该方案以极低训练成本把稀缺参考数据转化为可扩展的识别增益。

Abstract: Wrist-worn accelerometers make activity monitoring feasible at scale, but incidental arm motion, device orientation, and shifting context blur the signal that separates activities. Calibration studies supply higher-quality references such as concurrent thigh recordings or supervised laboratory wrist sessions, but they are expensive, cover few participants, and are rarely available at deployment. How can limited reference data improve recognition for participants who wear only a wrist device? We propose WearableBridge, which holds the reference encoder and classifier fixed and learns a task-guided transport map, the bridge, that carries each wrist feature into the region the classifier associates with its activity. Reference features or the classifier's predictions indicate the destination, and a classification loss penalizes wrist features that land in the region of another activity. We instantiate WearableBridge with three bridges: Mapper regresses onto reference features, Distillation matches the classifier's class probabilities, and MeanFlow learns a one-step flow from wrist toward reference features. On 40 held-out free-living PAAWS participants, all three bridges improve macro-F1 in every training run, and the strongest, MeanFlow, raises mean macro-F1 from 35.1% to 56.0% with concurrent thigh references (Thigh2Wrist), and from 54.0% to 63.5% with group-matched laboratory wrist references (Lab2Freeliving). Fitted only on PAAWS, MeanFlow also raises macro-F1 on the CAPTURE-24 cohort from 42.8% to 54.7%. The gains replicate on selfBACK, hold across four reference encoders, and grow with temporal context. A study can therefore collect reference recordings for a calibration subset and improve wrist-only recognition for every other participant, training only a bridge of 0.13 to 2.44 million parameters while the reference encoder and classifier stay unchanged.

</details>


### [134] [Large Language Model Deployment on Resource-Constrained Edge Devices: A Practitioners Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09075)
*Rahul Maliakkal,Yashasvi Makin,Plawan Rath,Rohit Jain,Ankur Sadhoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文是一篇综述，系统梳理使大语言模型（LLM）能够在资源受限的边缘设备上部署的最新软硬件技术，并阐述边缘部署的动机与优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽在AI多个领域带来变革，但其庞大的资源需求限制了在移动与边缘设备上的部署；将模型落到资源受限边缘端成为下一阶段的关键挑战。

Method: 文献综述方法：归纳近期软件优化（如压缩、量化、推理加速等方向）与专用硬件方面的创新，分析这些技术如何共同使边缘端LLM部署成为可能。

Result: 指出软件优化与专用硬件的结合已使LLM边缘部署具备可行性，并总结边缘部署的四项优势：本地数据处理、成本节约、时延降低与个性化响应。

Conclusion: LLM边缘部署在技术与收益层面均具可行性，是未来重要发展方向；软件与硬件协同优化是推进该方向的核心路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have transformed many areas of artificial intelligence (AI), but their substantial resource requirements limit their deployment on mobile and edge devices. Although LLMs have demonstrated impressive capabilities in various applications, the next challenge is deploying these models on resource-limited edge devices. Recent innovations in software optimizations and specialized hardware have made it feasible to deploy LLMs on edge devices. Edge deployment offers benefits including local data processing, cost savings, reduced latency, and personalized responses. This paper reviews the latest techniques that make the deployment of LLMs on edge devices feasible.

</details>


### [135] [BrainCoT: A Multi-Task Zero-Shot Brain Signal Foundation Model with Neurometric-Anchored Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09082)
*Yiyu Gui,Mingzhi Chen,Guibo Luo,Yuchao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BrainCoT——多任务零样本脑信号基础模型，结合神经度量锚定的思维链推理，实现无需下游数据适配的多任务零样本应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑信号存在显著的多中心异质性、强个体差异和高多样任务设定，导致模型难以跨任务泛化。现有脑信号基础模型虽依赖大规模预训练学习可泛化表征，但仍需目标数据特定的有监督适配，缺乏多任务零样本能力，且很少提供可验证的预测证据。

Method: 将异质任务统一为指令驱动框架，预训练后单模型可直接零样本应用于多任务；引入以神经度量证据为锚的思维链（CoT）实现可验证推理；采用决策一致性理由训练（Decision-Consistent Rationale Training）使过程学习与正确预测保持一致。

Result: 在广泛评估中，BrainCoT在无下游训练数据的零样本分类上取得平均ACC 67.41%、AUC 74.33%，相比使用1%下游标注数据训练的最强线性探针基线，ACC提升3.85%、AUC提升5.97%。

Conclusion: BrainCoT通过指令驱动统一框架与神经度量锚定思维链，填补了脑信号基础模型在零样本多任务与可验证推理方面的空白，且零样本性能已优于少量标注数据有监督适配的基线。

Abstract: Brain signals exhibit substantial multi-center heterogeneity, strong inter-subject variability, and highly diverse task settings, which makes it difficult to build models that generalize beyond a single task. While recent brain signal foundation models leverage large-scale pretraining to learn generalizable representations, they often fall short in practice because they still require target-data-specific supervised adaptation, lack multi-task zero-shot capability, and rarely provide verifiable evidence to support their predictions. In this work, we present BrainCoT, a multi-task zero-shot Brain signal foundation model with neurometric-anchored Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning. BrainCoT unifies heterogeneous tasks into an instruction-driven framework so that, after pretraining, a single model can be directly applied to multiple tasks in zero-shot settings without additional adaptation. It further introduces neurometric-anchored chain-of-thought grounded in neurometric evidence for verifiable reasoning, and adopts Decision-Consistent Rationale Training to keep process learning aligned with correct predictions. Across extensive evaluations, BrainCoT achieves an average ACC of 67.41% and AUC of 74.33% in zero-shot classification without downstream training data, outperforming the strongest linear-probing baseline trained with 1% labeled downstream data by 3.85% in ACC and 5.97% in AUC.

</details>


### [136] [TUGB: A Two-stage Uncertainty-aware Generative Auto-Bidding Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09091)
*Luzhan Yuan,Chao Mi,Xiahao Shi,Yi Mao,Shengsheng Niu,Xin Zhu,Jie He,Zhangang Lin,Ching Law*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TUGB提出两阶段不确定性感知生成式自动出价框架，将生成式序列建模与强化学习结合，在JD搜索广告全量上线实现成本+2.32%、目标成本+3.57%，并具备跨机制与跨场景泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Decision Transformer类生成式自动出价方法存在三方面缺陷：(1)在静态离线数据集中探索受限；(2)对动态环境变化脆弱；(3)依赖启发式return-to-go（RTG）设定，在未见流量上次优。

Method: 两阶段流水线：先预训练生成式出价模型（Generative Bidding Model），再通过基于集成的（ensemble-based）不确定性感知环境模型进行微调；以认知不确定性度量惩罚高风险轨迹，并将策略从RTG条件生成逐步演化为直接出价优化。

Result: 实验显著优于SOTA基线；JD搜索广告全量部署带来成本提升2.32%、目标成本提升3.57%，并在后续机制升级与JD外卖广告场景中成功适配。

Conclusion: 生成式建模与RL的两阶段融合、辅以不确定性惩罚，可有效缓解离线探索不足与环境漂移问题，兼具工业有效性与良好泛化性。

Abstract: Generative modeling, particularly Decision Transformers, shows promise in auto-bidding strategies. However, it struggles with limited exploration in static offline datasets, vulnerability to dynamic environmental shifts, and a reliance on heuristic return-to-go (RTG) specifications that may yield suboptimal performance on unseen traffic. To address these challenges, we propose the Two-stage Uncertainty-aware Generative Auto-Bidding (TUGB) framework. TUGB integrates generative sequence modeling with Reinforcement Learning (RL). It employs a two-stage pipeline where a pre-trained Generative Bidding Model is fine-tuned via an ensemble-based Uncertainty-aware Environment Model. This approach quantifies epistemic uncertainty to penalize high-risk trajectories and evolves the policy from RTG-conditioning to direct bidding optimization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TUGB significantly outperforms SOTA baselines. Notably, full-scale deployment on JD Search Ad achieved a 2.32\% increase in cost and 3.57\% in target cost, validating its industrial efficacy, while its successful adaptation to subsequent mechanism upgrades and JD Food Delivery Ad further validates TUGB's superior generalization capability.

</details>


### [137] [COMBIO: Automated Discovery of a Compact Composite Biomarker from Routine Laboratory Panels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09102)
*Tomer D. Meirman,Lior S. Rokach,Bracha Shapira,Seffi Cohen,Noa Dagan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COMBIO将梯度提升模型蒸馏为由5个检验指标乘幂构成的闭式复合生物标志物，在六个任务上与基于同五项指标的逻辑回归AUC差距不超过0.03，其价值在于紧凑可解释而非更高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于常规实验室面板的机器学习模型虽能预测结局，但可解释性差、难以在实际临床中落地运行，因此需要一个既保留预测能力又简洁透明的表达形式。

Method: 构建自动化流程：以梯度提升模型为参考，用SHAP值对指标排序并选出5个候选检验项、确定各指数允许的正负号；复合指标为各指标取整数次幂（-2到+2）后的连乘；在内层验证集上穷举小整数指数组合选式，再在独立测试集上评估。任务包括三项NHANES结局、印度肝病患者数据集肝病分类、MIMIC-IV院内死亡与脓毒症识别，后两者另在eICU数据库做外部验证。

Result: 六个测试集上，COMBIO与使用同样5项指标的逻辑回归相比AUC差异最大仅0.03；院内死亡复合指标在外部eICU数据库中AUC为0.698；仅使用源医院数据库常规可得指标的版本，在覆盖92%住院的队列中AUC为0.786。

Conclusion: 乘幂连乘形式主要带来紧凑与可解释性，而非精度提升；临床使用前仍需本地验证与再校准。

Abstract: Machine-learning models based on routine laboratory panels can predict outcomes but can be difficult to interpret and operationalize. We present COMBIO, an automated pipeline that distils such a model into a compact closed-form biomarker. The composite is a product of laboratory measurements raised to integer powers from minus two to two. The sign of each exponent indicates whether higher values of the corresponding measurement increase or decrease the composite. Shapley additive explanation values from the gradient-boosted reference model rank five candidate measurements and guide eligible exponent signs. An exhaustive search over small integer exponents selects the formula on an inner validation set, before evaluation on the reserved test set. We evaluated COMBIO across six tasks: three National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey outcomes, liver-disease classification in the Indian Liver Patient Dataset, and Medical Information Mart for Intensive Care IV in-hospital mortality and sepsis case-finding, with external evaluation in the eICU Collaborative Research Database for the two hospital tasks. Across the six test sets, COMBIO's area under the receiver operating characteristic curve differed by at most 0.03 from logistic regression using the five candidate laboratory measurements. The hospital mortality composite had an area under the curve of 0.698 in the external database. A version restricted to widely available measurements in the source hospital database achieved an area under the curve of 0.786 in a cohort covering 92% of admissions. The multiplicative representation provides compactness rather than additional accuracy, and local validation and recalibration are required before clinical use.

</details>


### [138] [Deep Learning Theory through the Lens of Statistical Physics: A Topical Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09138)
*Wei Huang,Youjin Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一篇从统计物理视角综述深度学习理论的topical review，用相变/临界性、自由能视角、核与高斯过程极限、平均场与动态平均场、随机矩阵、Fokker-Planck等概念与方法，统一刻画宽网络、损失景观、随机梯度训练动力学及过参数化与泛化等相变类现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型与深度学习的实证成功远超对其"如何学习与泛化"的理论理解；大规模网络具有高维、非线性、强耦合特征，恰好是统计物理擅长描述的对象，因此需要一个物理式框架来整合零散、甚至相互竞争的理论叙事。

Method: 采用统计物理思维方式与工具的综述性综合：(1) 概念层——相与临界性、自由能视角、普适性、标度与可解极限；(2) 方法层——核极限与高斯过程极限、平均场与动态平均场理论、随机矩阵工具、Fokker-Planck 形式；(3) 统一原则——明确各近似的适用范围与有效性区域。

Result: 系统梳理了四条主线：宽网络的平均场分析；损失景观的谱与几何视角；将随机梯度训练视为动力过程；以及用相变类比组织过参数化、泛化与表征变化等现象。并阐明各近似分别解释了什么、何时成立、彼此叙事如何关联或分歧。

Conclusion: 这些视角共同构成一个统一物理框架，将表征学习视为高维系统中的集体现象，桥接梯度优化的确定性极限与随机机制，为理解现代深度学习提供了带明确有效性边界的可解理论工具箱。

Abstract: The recent success of foundation models and deep learning has far outpaced our theoretical understanding of how large-scale neural networks learn and generalize. Statistical physics provides a natural language for describing such high-dimensional, nonlinear, and strongly coupled systems, offering concepts such as mean-field theory, phase transitions, energy landscapes, and stochastic dynamics. In this topical review, we adopt a statistical-physics way of thinking -- from concepts including phases and criticality, and free-energy viewpoints, to methods such as kernel and Gaussian-process limits, mean-field and dynamical mean-field theories, random-matrix tools, and Fokker-Planck formalisms, and to a mindset emphasizing universality, scaling, and tractable limits with explicit validity regimes. We survey recent progress on understanding modern deep learning through this lens, including mean-field analyses of wide networks, spectral and geometric perspectives on loss landscapes, stochastic-gradient-based training viewed as a dynamical process, and phase-transition analogies that organize phenomena such as overparameterization, generalization, and representation change. Our goal is to synthesize these threads into a coherent framework that clarifies what each approximation explains, when it is expected to hold, and how apparently competing narratives relate or diverge. Together, these perspectives form a unified physical framework that views representation learning as a collective phenomenon in high-dimensional systems, bridging deterministic and stochastic regimes of gradient-based optimization.

</details>


### [139] [Learning Generative Dynamics for 3D Molecule Generation via Sequential Neuro-Symbolic Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09142)
*Zhuoran Li,Xu Sun,Chang Wen Chen,Wanyu Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SensDiff框架，将符号化物理化学规则按"由粗到细"顺序、自适应加权地注入3D分子扩散模型的训练中，使生成样本在保持统计合理性的同时提升化学有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D分子扩散模型仅从数据中隐式学习原子分布，未显式编码物理化学规则于生成动力学中，导致生成样本统计上合理但化学上无效。

Method: 基于"3D分子扩散轨迹呈现粗到细演化"这一观察：先消除宏观几何冲突，再通过迭代去噪实现微观化学一致性。据此，SensDiff在训练中按序施加约束并采用自适应权重，逐步将生成导向有效分子几何；同时把领域知识转译为可解释的生成控制量，为不透明的神经去噪提供科学依据。

Result: 在分子基准与真实世界约束生成任务上，SensDiff持续提升化学有效性，同时满足物理化学规则与用户指定目标。

Conclusion: 顺序化、自适应加权的神经-符号约束可使扩散模型内化科学先验，兼顾生成质量、化学有效性与可解释性；代码已公开。

Abstract: Diffusion models have recently emerged as a powerful paradigm for generating 3D molecules. Despite showing remarkable promise, they learn atomic distributions implicitly from data, without explicitly encoding physicochemical rules into their generative dynamics. Consequently, generated samples can be statistically plausible yet chemically invalid. To address this problem, we propose Sequential Neuro-Symbolic Constrained Diffusion (SensDiff), a framework that integrates symbolic rules into generative modeling, enabling the model to internalize scientific priors during training. Central to SensDiff is the observation that the diffusion trajectory of 3D molecule generation exhibits a coarse-to-fine evolution: valid molecules arise by first mitigating macroscopic geometric conflicts and then refining structures toward microscopic chemical consistency through iterative denoising. Accordingly, SensDiff mirrors this dynamic by sequentially enforcing constraints with adaptive weighting during training, progressively steering generation toward valid molecular geometries. Moreover, SensDiff translates domain knowledge into interpretable generative controls, grounding opaque neural denoising in scientific principles. Experiments on molecular benchmarks and real-world constrained generation tasks confirm that SensDiff consistently improves chemical validity while adhering to physicochemical rules and user-specified targets. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/WanyuGroup/NeurIPS2026_SensDiff.

</details>


### [140] [Augmentron: Scalable Multi-View Visual Augmentation for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09145)
*Tanay Tandon,Aseem Doriwala,Jade Choghari,Catherine Weaver,Pragna Mannam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Augmentron：一种几何感知的数据增强流水线，通过生成可复用的场景表征并按录制的相机位姿渲染，产出时间与多视角一致的环境/工作空间变化，同时保留机器人、任务物体、动作与标注；在三个真实操作任务中，用其数据替换一半真实训练数据即可提升分布外干扰物与工作空间变化下的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉多样性狭窄的演示数据训练出的机器人策略，容易对环境中的任务无关特征敏感。现有生成式增强方法虽能增加视觉多样性，但逐帧、逐视角独立处理计算开销大，且跨相机与跨时间步的观测可能不一致。

Method: 提出 Augmentron 几何感知流水线：先生成可复用的场景表征（几何一致的三维/场景表示），再通过录制的相机位姿进行渲染，从而获得时间一致、多视角一致的环境与工作空间变化；同时保持机器人本体、任务物体、动作及标注不变。

Result: 在三个真实机器人操作任务上，用 Augmentron 数据替换一半真实训练数据后，三类任务在分布外干扰物与分布外工作空间变化评估下成功率均提升；与其它增强技术的单独对比中，Augmentron 在全部三种条件下取得最高的合并成功率。

Conclusion: 将增强数据引入训练配方有助于策略泛化到动态的真实世界场景；几何感知、跨视角与跨时间一致的渲染式增强是提升机器人策略鲁棒性的有效手段。

Abstract: Robot policies trained on visually narrow demonstrations can become sensitive to environmental features that are unrelated to the task. Existing generative augmentation methods can introduce visual diversity, but processing every frame and camera view independently is computationally expensive and may produce inconsistent observations across cameras and timestamps. We introduce Augmentron, a geometry-aware pipeline that generates reusable scene representations and renders them through recorded camera poses. This produces temporally and multi-view consistent environ- ment and workspace variations while preserving the robot, task objects, actions, and annotations. Across three physical manipulation tasks, replacing half of the real world training dataset with Augmentron data improves model success across three tasks for both in out-of-distribution distractor objects, and out-of-distribution workspace variation evaluations. In a separate comparison against alternate augmentation techniques, Augmentron achieves the highest pooled success in all three conditions. The results show that introducing augmented data to a data training recipe can help policies generalize to dynamic real-world settings.

</details>


### [141] [Beyond the Grid: Continuous Dictionary Pursuit for Interpretable Signal Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09163)
*Nick Janssen,Melanie Schaller,Bodo Rosenhahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出连续字典追踪（CDP）框架，通过对异构闭式函数原子进行连续流形上的梯度优化，实现无网格失配、可解释的信号分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习信号处理精度高但难以解释；经典匹配追踪与稀疏编码透明但受频谱泄漏和网格失配困扰（信号频率与离散字典原子不对齐）。且已有连续追踪方法仅针对单一解析函数族。

Method: CDP直接在数据上学习闭式函数参数：在可微原子的连续流形上优化，搜索涵盖周期、趋势、瞬态与不连续结构的异构原子库；采用基于数据驱动先验与梯度优化的贪心迭代策略逐个分离成分；对不连续波形用谱锐度退火使其可微；以预设原子预算和残差收敛作为停止准则保证稀疏重建。

Result: 在合成与真实数据上：消除库内原子族的网格离散化误差；平稳合成信号上生成参数复原误差在1%以内；稀疏场景下在经典方法、连续追踪与神经网络基线中取得最佳稀疏—精度权衡；提取原子可作为结构化课程目标训练序列模型，在多数数据集上提升预测精度。

Conclusion: CDP提供了一种"白箱"式信号分解替代方案，兼具高重建保真度与直接物理可解释性，并可向下游序列建模迁移。

Abstract: Deep learning models achieve high accuracy in signal processing but remain difficult to interpret. Classical matching pursuit and sparse coding offer better transparency but suffer from spectral leakage and grid mismatch when signal frequencies do not align with discrete dictionary atoms. We introduce Continuous Dictionary Pursuit (CDP), a framework for interpretable signal decomposition that learns the parameters of closed-form functions directly from data. CDP optimizes over a continuous manifold of differentiable atoms and, unlike prior continuous pursuit methods that operate on a single analytic family, searches a heterogeneous library spanning periodic, trend, transient, and discontinuous structures. The algorithm employs a greedy iterative strategy using data-driven priors and gradient-based optimization to isolate individual signal components, with discontinuous waveforms made differentiable through spectral sharpness annealing. To ensure a sparse atom set for reconstruction, the framework utilizes a stopping criterion based on a predefined atom budget and residual error convergence. Evaluations on synthetic and real-world datasets show that CDP removes the grid discretization error for atom families contained in its library, recovers generative parameters to within $1\%$ on stationary synthetic signals expressible in that library, and achieves the best sparsity-accuracy trade-off among classical, continuous-pursuit, and neural baselines in the sparse regime. Beyond decomposition, we demonstrate that the extracted atoms can serve as structured curriculum targets for training sequence models, improving forecasting accuracy over standard end-to-end training on most evaluated datasets. The method offers a glass-box alternative for signal decomposition by providing high reconstruction fidelity alongside direct physical interpretability.

</details>


### [142] [TOFS: Feature Selection with Swap Supervision for Semantic Communication over Fading Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09173)
*Zhiyi Zhu,Eiji Takimoto,Patrick Finnerty,Chikara Ohta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出任务导向特征选择框架TOFS，以接收端分类损失为准则学习特征优先级排序，在单次前向中输出选择结果，CIFAR-10上性能优于基于重要性的基线及SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务导向语义通信在有限通信资源与恶劣信道下传输任务相关特征以支撑接收端推理。对图像分类而言，某一特征的有用性依赖于其他已传特征以及无线信道引入的失真，因此有效特征优先化须以所选特征传输后的分类性能为依据，而现有基于重要性度量的选择无法刻画特征间互补性与信道失真影响。

Method: 构建TOFS（Task-Oriented Feature Selection）框架：以接收端分类损失评估特征替换的效果，在独立传输样本下确认的损失差值构成成对排序监督信号，用于训练选择器网络；训练后的选择器可在一次前向传播中预测各特征优先级，从而完成特征选择。

Result: CIFAR-10仿真显示，在相同传输特征数与平均发射功率下，TOFS的分类准确率优于基于重要性的特征选择基线；在Rayleigh衰落与高斯噪声下达到84%准确率，而SOTA方法为68%。

Conclusion: 以端到端分类损失差值为监督信号的特征优先级学习能有效刻画特征间依赖与信道失真，TOFS在单次前向推理下实现高效特征选择，显著提升任务导向传输的分类鲁棒性；其效果目前基于CIFAR-10仿真验证。

Abstract: Task-oriented semantic communication transmits task-relevant features to support receiver inference under limited communication resources and adverse channel conditions. For image classification, the usefulness of a feature depends on the other transmitted features and the distortion introduced by the wireless channel. Effective feature prioritization should therefore account for classification performance after transmission of the selected features. This paper proposes a Task-Oriented Feature Selection (\textit{TOFS}) framework and method, which evaluates feature replacements using receiver classification loss. Loss differences confirmed under independent transmission samples provide pairwise ranking supervision for a selector network. The trained selector predicts priorities in a single forward pass. CIFAR-10 simulations demonstrate improved classification accuracy over an importance-based feature selection baseline at the same transmitted feature count and average transmit power. The proposed \textit{TOFS} framework shows 84\% accuracy performance under Rayleigh fading and Gaussian noise, compared with the 68\% performance of a SOTA method.

</details>


### [143] [SIFR: Stable Signed Flow Reinforcement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09175)
*Runlong Liao,Xingqian Xu,Lizhang Chen,Baiyu Su,Yufan Huang,Zhonghao Wang,Akash Gokul,Ming-Yu Liu,Qiang Liu,Yogesh Balaji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Stable Signed Flow Reinforcement（SIFR）：用带符号优势替代正奖励权重以补上"降低低奖励样本概率"的信号，并用参考模型的速度漂移约束（拉格朗日乘子自适应调整正则强度、固定漂移预算）保证稳定，可统一解释现有 flow RL 方法；在 2.5B–64B 多骨干、12 组骨干–奖励组合上匹配或超越现有方法，奖励上升更快且长训练不塌缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于奖励的 flow 模型微调需同时提升高奖励、压低低奖励样本，并在多轮迭代更新中保持稳定。迭代式奖励微调虽简单，但正奖励权重只做"加权拟合"，缺少明确的不拟合（unfitting）信号；反复重加权会使分布收敛集中、最终塌缩。

Method: 1）将奖励权重替换为带符号优势（signed advantage），为低奖励样本提供显式的抑制梯度；2）对相对参考模型的速度漂移施加约束，用拉格朗日乘子把正则强度自适应调节到预设漂移预算；3）给出理论刻画：现有 flow RL 方法均可归入该框架，本质是隐式最小化 signed loss，区别仅在于正则项选择。

Result: 在 SD3.5 Medium（2.5B）、FLUX.2 Klein（9B）上验证，并扩展到 Cosmos 3 Super Text2Image（64B）；在偏好型与规则型奖励共 12 组骨干–奖励组合上，均匹配或超过现有方法，达到其峰值奖励的速度显著更快，且在其出现平台或塌缩的长程训练中仍持续提升。

Conclusion: 带符号优势 + 漂移预算约束构成一个简洁且可扩展的 flow 强化微调框架，兼具稳定性与持续增益，并可作为统一视角理解既有方法。

Abstract: Reward-based fine-tuning of flow models requires effectively promoting high-reward and suppressing low-reward samples, while maintaining training stability over many rounds of iterative updates. Iterative reward fine-tuning provides a simple starting point, but the positive reward weights give no explicit unfitting signal, and repeated reweighting concentrates the distribution and causes collapse. We propose Stable Signed Flow Reinforcement (SIFR), which replaces the reward weights by signed advantages to supply the missing unfitting signal, and constrains the velocity drift from a reference model to stabilize the updates, with a Lagrange multiplier that adapts the regularization strength to a prescribed drift budget. We further show that existing flow RL methods fall within this framework: they implicitly minimize the signed loss, differing in their regularization choices. We validate SIFR on SD3.5 Medium (2.5B) and FLUX.2 Klein (9B) and scale it to Cosmos 3 Super Text2Image (64B): across all 12 backbone--reward pairs with preference and rule-based rewards, it matches or exceeds existing methods, reaches their peak rewards much faster, and keeps improving over long training where they plateau or collapse.

</details>


### [144] [A Taxonomy-Driven Synthetic Dataset for Retrieval Effectiveness and Fairness Evaluation in Hiring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09178)
*Warren Jouanneau,Morgane Hoffmann,Emma Jouffroy,Marc Palyart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个基于分类体系、完全合成的多语言HR数据集与基准，同时评测检索效果与算法公平性；基于66万真实自由职业者画像构建，含候选人档案、项目简述与人口属性反事实孪生；审计显示SOTA嵌入模型表现偏离MTEB通用分数，语法性别语言中存在公平性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习招聘工具已普遍应用，但隐私法规与商业敏感性使公开基准构建在法律上复杂，导致HR检索与公平性缺乏可用、可公开的评测资源。

Method: 构建分类体系驱动的完全合成HR数据集：以66万真实自由职业者画像为基础生成多语言语料，包含候选人档案、项目简述以及反事实人口属性孪生样本；随后对最先进嵌入模型进行检索效果与公平性双重审计，并与通用MTEB分数对比。

Result: 嵌入模型在HR语料上的表现与通用MTEB得分出现明显偏离；在语法性别语言（如含性别屈折的语言）中观察到公平性差异；表明通用基准不足以支撑HR场景评估。

Conclusion: HR场景需要定制化评测以识别在语义准确性与公平性之间取得帕累托最优的模型；合成数据可规避隐私与法律障碍，为招聘检索与公平性研究提供可复现基准。

Abstract: While machine learning hiring tools are ubiquitous, the need for privacy regulations and commercial sensitivity makes public benchmarking legally complex. We address this challenge by introducing a taxonomy-driven, fully synthetic HR dataset and benchmark to simultaneously evaluate retrieval effectiveness and algorithmic fairness. Grounded in 660K real freelancer profiles, our multilingual corpus features candidate profiles, project briefs, and counterfactual demographic twins. Auditing state-of-the-art embedding models reveals performance divergence from general MTEB scores, alongside fairness disparities in grammatically gendered languages. This demonstrates that general benchmarks are insufficient for HR, requiring tailored evaluations to identify Pareto-optimal models that balance semantic accuracy and fairness.

</details>


### [145] [RoleWalk: training-free structural embeddings for directed graphs that transfer across graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09189)
*Maixent Chenebaux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RoleWalk 是一种免训练的结构角色嵌入方法：从每个节点出发做短程惰性随机游走，记录转移概率特征函数与到起点距离的分布，在有向图上用"前向+后向"游走刻画目标/源共享关系；嵌入仅依赖固定半径邻域，坐标跨图语义一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构角色嵌入存在两类缺陷：一类（struc2vec、Role2Vec、RESD）学到的坐标只对单一图有效；另一类（GraphWave、Digraphwave）从全图读取尺度与归一化，导致相同邻域在不同图中得到不同坐标。此外，少数方法利用边方向信息。作者希望得到一种跨图可迁移、含义一致且能处理有向图的结构角色嵌入。

Method: RoleWalk 无需训练，对每个节点执行短程惰性随机游走，并在每一步记录两个分布：(1) 转移概率的分布，通过其在固定频率网格上的特征函数表示；(2) 游走者到起始节点的距离分布。在有向图上，采用先向前再向后的游走以捕获共享目标或共享来源的邻域关系。理论分析给出三项性质：无曲率时它是游走"足迹"的核均值嵌入（kernel mean embedding）；它能区分 Weisfeiler-Leman 测试无法区分的图；剪枝小概率时具有逐节点误差证书。嵌入只依赖固定半径内的邻域。

Result: 在五个分类基准上，RoleWalk 与最佳角色嵌入方法相当，但开销仅为其一小部分；在 Wikipedia adminship 真实有向角色任务上取得最高分；在 Enron 网络对齐基准的每个噪声水平上都超过已发表的 Digraphwave 精度；中心性预测器迁移到四倍大规模图上的效果优于现有归纳式嵌入、手工特征与训练好的 GIN；单 CPU 核上可在 20 秒至 3 分钟内嵌入一百万个节点。

Conclusion: RoleWalk 提供了跨图语义一致、支持有向图、免训练且高效的结构角色嵌入，理论与实证均显示其在精度、可迁移性与计算成本上的综合优势，并已以 BSD 许可开源。

Abstract: Structural role embeddings describe a node by its position in a graph - hub, bridge, periphery, member of a dense core - rather than by who its neighbours are. Existing methods either learn coordinates specific to one graph (struc2vec, Role2Vec, RESD) or read scales and normalisations from the whole graph (GraphWave, Digraphwave), so the same neighbourhood can receive different coordinates in two graphs; few read edge directions. We present RoleWalk, a training-free embedding that follows a short lazy random walk from each node and records, at each step, two distributions: that of the transition probabilities, through its characteristic function at a fixed grid of frequencies, and that of the walker's distance to its starting node. On directed graphs, walks that go forward then backward add who shares a node's targets or sources. The embedding depends only on a node's neighbourhood within a fixed radius, so its coordinates mean the same thing in every graph. We show that, without curvature, it is the kernel mean embedding of the walk's footprint, that it separates graphs that the Weisfeiler-Leman test cannot, and that pruning small probabilities comes with a per-node error certificate. Empirically, RoleWalk is competitive with the best role embeddings on five classification benchmarks at a fraction of their cost, has the highest scores on real directed roles in Wikipedia adminship, exceeds the published accuracy of Digraphwave at every noise level of its own Enron network-alignment benchmark, transfers centrality predictors to graphs four times larger better than existing inductive embeddings, hand-made features and a trained GIN, and embeds a million nodes in 20 s to 3 min on one CPU core. The code is open source (BSD license): https://github.com/kerighan/rolewalk

</details>


### [146] [Copies Are Not Confirmations: How News Duplication Fools LLM Judges and Continual Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09203)
*Abdelkader Metales,Miriam Grabenheinrich,Aicha Metales*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 新闻中的重复报道（通讯社稿、集团内共享、通稿）被LLM判断器与持续学习器误当作独立确认。通过MinHash检测副本链，发现重复呈送会大幅提高模型接受虚假信息的比例；按链去重可将接受复制谎言降低0.10–0.24，代价不超过4.2个百分点的真话接受率；而单纯计数或依赖来源提示并不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量新闻报道实为同一来源的复制（wire stories、媒体集团内共享、辛迪加稿件与新闻通稿）。作者关注的核心问题是：LLM判断器（judge）与持续学习者是否会把这类副本误认为"独立的多方确认"，从而在评估真实性时被放大误导。

Method: 1）在20万篇英文CC-News上，用MinHash检测器识别近重复（去样板后精确率0.97，18.9%被链到另一媒体的近副本）。2）实验操控：将同一主张的三份完全相同转载 vs 一份报告呈现给判断器，考察对未知人物的谎言接受率。3）检验对编辑转载、LLM改写（phi4、Llama 3.2、Gemini 2.5 Flash）的检测能力。4）模拟持续学习流：每条检测到的复制链只呈现一份报告。5）针对改写副本，提出"命名实体缺失"检验（报告中的命名实体在更早相似文本中缺失的比例），与实体重叠、TF-IDF、嵌入模型比较，并以生成式改写网络的漏检模式加以修正。

Result: 检测器链到92%的人工编辑转载原文，但未链到任何LLM改写。三份相同转载使Llama 3.2 3B接受谎言比例由0.06升至0.71，phi4 14B由0.21升至0.90，且二者忽略"来自同一来源"的提示；Gemini 2.5 Flash单份即信，Gemini 2.5 Pro能遵循来源提示与表格。持续学习模拟中，每链仅呈一份使四类判断器接受复制谎言降低0.10–0.24，真话接受率平均损失≤4.2个百分点。实体缺失检验区分LLM改写与100份同事件独立报告（AUC 0.98–0.99，误报1份）；仅实体重叠即可达到相近效果，TF-IDF与当前嵌入模型略逊；该检验对自然同源衍生文本检出有限，但结合改写网络漏检模式后可将接受改写谎言从0.67降至0.20（改写者虚构细节时为0.40）。

Conclusion: 仅统计报告数量或链数量不是可靠防御，来源/出处信息的效果高度依赖具体判断器，而"串通来源"（colluding sources）仍是未解问题。作者开源了代码与标注数据。

Abstract: Many news reports of a claim are copies of one source: wire stories, content shared within a media group, syndicated features and press releases. We ask whether LLM judges and continual learners treat such copies as independent confirmations. In 200,000 English CC-News articles, a MinHash detector links 18.9% to a near-copy from another outlet (precision 0.97 once boilerplate is removed); it links 92% of edited reprints to their originals but none of the LLM rewrites by phi4, Llama 3.2 or Gemini 2.5 Flash. Shown three identical reprints of a claim about an unknown person instead of one report, Llama 3.2 3B and phi4 14B raised the share of lies they accepted from 0.06 to 0.71 and from 0.21 to 0.90 and ignored a note that the reports came from one source; Gemini 2.5 Flash already believed a single report, and Gemini 2.5 Pro followed provenance notes and tables. In a simulated continual-learning stream, showing the judge one report per detected chain cut the copied lies it accepted by 0.10-0.24 across four judges, at a mean cost of no more than 4.2 percentage points of accepted truths. Against reworded copies, a test of how many of a report's named entities are absent from an earlier look-alike separates LLM rewrites from 100 labelled independent reports of the same event with AUC 0.98-0.99 and wrongly flags one; entity overlap alone does as well, TF-IDF and a current embedding model come close, and the test catches few natural same-source derivatives. With the miss patterns of generated rewrite networks, it cut the reworded-copy lies accepted from 0.67 to 0.20 (0.40 when the rewriter invents details). Counting reports or chains alone is not a reliable defence, the effect of provenance information depends on the judge, and colluding sources remain the open problem. Code and labels: https://github.com/Metalesaek/copies-experiment

</details>


### [147] [Provable State Estimation with Recurrent Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09217)
*Vincent Andrieu,Pauline Bernard,Lucas Brivadis,Laurent Praly,Christian Wolf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从非线性观测器理论视角分析循环神经网络（RNN），通过将RNN视为连续潜动力系统（Neural ODE）的时间离散化，提出对潜流施加收缩条件即可保证存在从真实物理状态到潜空间的平滑单射映射，从而使该架构成为可调观测器：增大单一标量增益即可使估计误差指数收敛至任意小邻域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环模型在机器人状态估计中经验上有效，但缺乏关于收敛性与鲁棒性的形式化保证。作者希望填补这一理论与实践之间的鸿沟，用非线性观测器理论为RNN类架构提供收敛性与鲁棒性的理论支撑。

Method: 将RNN框架化为连续潜动力系统（Neural ODE）的时间离散化；对潜流施加收缩（contraction）条件；证明该条件下存在真实物理状态到潜空间的平滑单射映射；通过增大缩放潜向量场的单一标量增益来调节收敛速度；给出解码潜状态所需MLP复杂度的定量界；并将形式化保证推广到离散时间残差RNN实现；最后在真实移动机器人任务上验证，与vanilla GRU对比。

Result: 理论上证明了该架构构成可调观测器，估计误差可以指数速率收敛至零的任意小邻域；给出了解码潜状态所需MLP复杂度的定量界，并将保证扩展到离散时间残差RNN。实验上，可证明收缩的模型在真实移动机器人任务中优于vanilla GRU，训练更稳定；同时观察到未加约束的GRU在该任务上也收敛到收缩动力学。

Conclusion: 收缩性为RNN-based观测器提供了可证明的收敛与鲁棒性保证，且收缩可能是训练良好的序列模型的一种涌现特性，这为设计具有形式化保证的循环状态估计器提供了理论依据与实践启示。

Abstract: Estimating the state of a dynamical system from observations is a crucial task in robotics and control. Despite their empirical success, recurrent models often lack formal guarantees on convergence and robustness. We bridge this gap by analyzing these architectures from the perspective of nonlinear observer theory. By framing RNNs as a time-discretization of continuous latent dynamical systems (Neural ODEs), we demonstrate that enforcing a contraction condition on the latent flow guarantees the existence of a smooth, injective map of the true physical state into the latent space. We prove that this architecture acts as a tunable observer: by increasing a single scalar gain that scales the latent vector field, the estimation error can be driven to an arbitrarily small neighborhood of zero at an exponentially fast rate. We further establish quantitative bounds on the MLP complexity required to decode this latent state, and extend our formal guarantees to the discrete-time residual RNN implementation. We validate the theory on a real mobile robotic task, where a provably contracting model outperforms a vanilla GRU with substantially more stable training. We also report that unconstrained GRUs converge to contracting dynamics on this task, suggesting that contraction is an emergent property of well-trained sequence models.

</details>


### [148] [Cross-Layer TopK: Dynamically Allocating Sparsity Across Layers in Transcoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09233)
*Anyuan Zhuo,Shaohua Zhang,Tianle Li,Yuan Lin,Diandian Gu,Jun Wang,Pinyan Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Cross-Layer TopK，用全模型统一的 L0 预算并在训练中动态分配到各层，使 CLT 重建更优、稀疏度更低、电路追踪更完整，并可结合稀疏解码获得 2.9x 训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨层转码器（CLT）可把大模型激活分解为可解释特征并追踪跨层电路，但训练困难：既要权衡稀疏性与重建质量，又需同时建模所有层。JumpReLU 只能通过惩罚项间接控制稀疏性；TopK 的直接扩展则对每层固定选择相同数量的隐变量。然而各层重建难度差异很大、中间层显著更难，等额预算并非最优。

Method: 提出 Cross-Layer TopK：为整个模型设定单一 L0 预算，并在训练过程中在各层之间动态分配该预算（而非每层固定 TopK 数量）。在 Qwen3-8B-Base 上与 JumpReLU 及其他 TopK 变体进行对比，并评估电路追踪（replacement、completeness 指标）、与稀疏解码的结合以及潜变量规模与稀疏性上的缩放定律。

Result: 相比 JumpReLU，FVU 更低且活跃隐变量少 20%；重建改善延续到电路追踪，获得更完整的电路与更高的 replacement 和 completeness 分数；每 token 固定预算便于与稀疏解码配合，减少计算与通信，每训练步提速 2.9x；并在潜变量规模与稀疏性两个维度上呈现缩放定律。

Conclusion: 跨层共享并动态分配稀疏预算优于逐层等额预算，是训练 CLT 更高效、更有效的方案，同时利于下游电路分析与工程加速；作者已开源 CLT 权重（HuggingFace: ByteDance-Seed/Cross-Layer-TopK-CLT）。

Abstract: Cross-Layer Transcoders (CLTs) decompose the activations of large language models into human-interpretable features and trace how these features interact across layers to form circuits, making them a key tool for mechanistic interpretability. However, training CLTs effectively and efficiently is hard. Beyond the usual trade-off between sparsity and reconstruction quality, a CLT has to model all layers at once, which adds a further challenge. The common JumpReLU recipe controls sparsity only indirectly through a penalty, while the direct extension of TopK selects a fixed number of latents in every layer. Yet reconstruction difficulty differs widely across layers, and intermediate layers are much harder than others, so giving every layer the same budget is suboptimal. We propose Cross-Layer TopK, which sets a single L0 budget for the whole model and dynamically allocates it across layers during training. In a comparison with JumpReLU and other TopK variants on Qwen3-8B-Base, this gives lower FVU while using 20% fewer active latents than JumpReLU. The better reconstruction carries over to circuit tracing, yielding more complete circuits with higher replacement and completeness scores. The fixed per-token budget of Cross-Layer TopK also makes it easy to pair with sparse decoding, which cuts both computation and communication and gives a 2.9x speedup per training step. We further show that Cross-Layer TopK follows scaling laws in both latent size and sparsity. We have open-sourced the weights of our CLT at https://huggingface.co/ByteDance-Seed/Cross-Layer-TopK-CLT.

</details>


### [149] [Controllable Molecule Generation via Sparse Representation Editing: An Interpretability-Driven Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09235)
*Zhuoran Li,Xu Sun,Chang Wen Chen,Wanyu Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SpaRE框架，通过在学习到的过完备稀疏特征空间中直接编辑LLM表示，实现基于大语言模型的分子生成的可解释细粒度可控控制（局部原子/官能团控制与全局性质控制）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在分子生成中虽有潜力，但其稠密、纠缠的表示难以对分子的特定子结构或性质进行精确控制；现有可解释性多停留于事后的分析层面。

Method: 学习一个过完备稀疏特征空间，将LLM的表示解耦为一组紧凑的、对应化学有意义概念的潜在特征；在该空间内直接操纵这些概念对齐的潜在特征，实现（1）局部控制：在指定位置生成目标原子与官能团；（2）全局控制：在给定范围内定制整体结构与理化性质。

Result: 实验表明SpaRE能在真实场景的复杂约束下生成化学上理想的分子，并为定量结构-性质分析提供机理层面的洞见。

Conclusion: 该框架将可解释性从事后分析推进到可操作的生成控制，实现细粒度、精确的可控分子生成，并提供机制性解释。

Abstract: Controllable molecule generation is crucial for diverse scientific applications, such as drug discovery and materials design. While large language models (LLMs) show great promise, their dense and entangled representations impede precise control over the generation of molecules with bespoke substructures or properties. To address this, we propose Sparse Representation Editing (SpaRE), an interpretability-driven framework for fine-grained and precise control in LLM-based molecule generation. The crux of SpaRE is to learn an overcomplete sparse feature space that disentangles LLM representations into a compact set of latent features corresponding to chemically meaningful concepts. Within this space, we can directly manipulate these concept-aligned latent features to achieve (1) local control, by generating target atoms and functional groups at specified positions; and (2) global control, by customizing the overall structural and physicochemical properties within defined ranges. In this way, our framework advances interpretability from post-hoc analysis to actionable generative control. Experiments show that SpaRE can generate chemically desirable molecules under complex constraints in real-world scenarios, while offering mechanistic insights for quantitative structure-property analysis. The code and demo are available at https://github.com/WanyuGroup/ICML2026_SpaRE.

</details>


### [150] [Practical Non-Stationary Graph Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09247)
*K. S. Sesh Kumar,Viacheslav Borovitskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出方差耦合高斯过程（VCGP），用正辅助信号经单一学习耦合指数调制图交互，兼顾异质网络的灵活性与参数复杂度，并在结构假设下刻画其唯一平稳重标定点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图上高斯过程可做预测与不确定性估计，但异质网络中节点变异性含义模糊：可能是有用信号（应传播），也可能是不可靠测量（应抑制），甚至两者混合；需要在灵活性与参数复杂度间取得平衡。

Method: 引入正辅助信号，通过单一学习的耦合指数调制图交互，控制高变异节点的信息传播或衰减；给出按簇扩展以容纳同一图中的多种耦合机制；在给定结构假设下推导基本族的唯一平稳重标定点——此处相关性与平稳图核一致而节点相关方差保留。

Result: 在交通、空气质量与天气数据集上，预测与不确定性估计相较平稳及逐节点非平稳基线具竞争力；在需要多种耦合机制的网络上涨幅最大；部分数据上方差重标定后的平稳相关已足够；性能依赖辅助信号与模型选择。

Conclusion: VCGP以极少参数实现节点依赖方差与平稳相关性的结合，是多机制异质网络的有效方案，但效果受辅助信号质量与模型选择影响。

Abstract: Gaussian processes on graphs provide a principled framework for prediction and uncertainty estimation, but modelling heterogeneous networks often requires balancing flexibility against parameter complexity. Variability at a node may reflect useful signal that should propagate through the network, unreliable measurements whose influence should be suppressed, or a mixture of both. We introduce Variance-Coupled Gaussian Processes (VCGPs), which use a positive auxiliary signal to modulate graph interactions through a single learned coupling exponent. The exponent controls how strongly high-variability nodes propagate or attenuate information, while a cluster-wise extension accommodates different regimes within the same graph. Under stated structural assumptions, we characterize the unique stationary-rescaling point of the basic family, where correlations match those of a stationary graph kernel while node-dependent variances remain. Experiments on traffic, air-quality and weather datasets show competitive prediction and uncertainty estimation relative to stationary and per-node non-stationary baselines, with the largest gains on networks requiring multiple coupling regimes. Stationary correlations after variance rescaling remain sufficient on some data, and performance depends on the auxiliary signal and model selection.

</details>


### [151] [Topology as a Design Variable: Joint Architecture-Sizing Optimization for SRAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09248)
*Can Wang,Junhao Ma,Yikai Wang,Shan Shen,Wei W. Xing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出开源工具 OpenYieldV2，首次将 SRAM 位单元拓扑作为可优化变量，实现 6T/10T 拓扑与晶体管尺寸的联合多目标优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SRAM 的 PPA-稳定性权衡取决于位单元拓扑，而拓扑是离散架构变量，与连续晶体管尺寸存在不可分离的耦合。但现有开源工具均不把拓扑作为可优化变量：OpenRAM 只生成版图不做优化，OpenYield 仅支持固定 6T 单元的标量尺寸优化。

Method: 三项集成贡献：(1) 全参数化编译器生成含外围电路与 replica-bitline 自定时（self-timing）的完整 6T 与 10T SRAM macro，使拓扑可在相同仿真条件下比较；(2) 少样本 TabPFN 代理模型，用有限的全 macro SPICE 样本学习拓扑相关的 PPA 响应；(3) 由粗到精的多目标优化器，结合离散架构选择的全局探索与连续晶体管尺寸的局部精炼。

Result: 在不同设计条件下的实验揭示出随工作约束移动的拓扑决策边界，而非固定设计规则；跨该边界联合优化相比最强单拓扑基线，超体积（hypervolume）最多提升 17.3%。

Conclusion: 将拓扑纳入优化空间可带来显著收益，拓扑选择应随工况自适应；OpenYieldV2 开源以支持可复现的 SRAM 设计研究。

Abstract: SRAM's power-performance-area-stability trade-off depends on bitcell topology, a discrete architectural choice that interacts non-separably with continuous transistor sizing. However, no open-source SRAM tool treats topology as an optimizable variable. OpenRAM generates SRAM layouts but performs no optimization, while OpenYield supports high-fidelity evaluation but restricts optimization to scalar sizing of a fixed 6T cell. OpenYieldV2 closes this gap through three integrated contributions. First, a fully parameterized compiler generates complete 6T and 10T SRAM macros with peripheral circuits and replica-bitline self-timing, enabling topology comparison under identical simulation conditions. Second, a few-shot TabPFN surrogate learns topology-dependent PPA responses with limited full-macro SPICE samples. Third, a coarse-to-refined multi-objective optimizer combines global exploration of discrete architectural choices with local refinement of continuous transistor sizes. Experiments across varying design conditions reveal a topology decision boundary that shifts with operating constraints rather than following a fixed design rule. Jointly optimizing across this boundary improves hypervolume by up to 17.3\% over the strongest single-topology baseline. OpenYieldV2 is open-source to support reproducible SRAM design research.

</details>


### [152] [Rethinking MoE Load Balancing as Feedback Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09252)
*Yikun Hou,Alp Yurtsever*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将MoE负载均衡重新表述为反馈控制问题，提出PID-Balancer控制族，并证明纯积分项（PID(I)）是唯一能消除持续失衡并抑制负载波动的成员；在混合比例切换与持续预训练两种非平稳场景下，PID(I)比辅助损失和loss-free方法均衡更稳、恢复更快，困惑度相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE训练通常是非平稳的：课程边界导致数据混合变化、持续预训练进入新领域或语料刷新，都会破坏路由器已收敛的专家负载分配。然而现有均衡方法只在平稳数据上被评估，缺乏对非平稳场景的针对性设计。

Method: 把每个专家负载偏离公平份额的量视为控制误差，把每专家路由偏置视为控制输入，基于经典PID的比例、积分、微分项定义PID-Balancer控制族。指出辅助均衡损失相当于比例控制器，loss-free偏置更新相当于对误差符号作用的积分控制器。将路由器视为带一步延迟的静态映射进行分析，推导出积分项是唯一同时消除持续失衡并对逐步负载波动取平均的项，比例与微分项会把波动传递或放大到路由偏置中。故采用单增益的纯积分形式PID(I)。

Result: 两种设置：(i) 从头训练OLMoE骨干（每层16至64专家），中途切换数据混合；(ii) 将已发布的OLMoE-1B-7B在新语料上持续预训练。PID(I)在切换前维持均衡，切换后比辅助损失与loss-free均衡恢复得更紧致，且困惑度相当。

Conclusion: 负载均衡应被视为反馈控制问题；在非平稳MoE训练中，纯积分控制器因其消除稳态误差并平滑负载噪声的能力而优于比例式辅助损失和符号式积分偏置更新，是一种简单（单增益）、可解释且更鲁棒的均衡方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) training is often not stationary: the data mixture changes at curriculum boundaries, when a model is continually pretrained into a new domain, or when the corpus is refreshed. Routing is learned against the current mixture, so each such change disrupts the per-expert load the router had converged to, and existing balancing methods have only been evaluated on stationary data. We rethink load balancing as a feedback control problem, in which the deviation of each expert's load from its fair share is the control error and the per-expert routing bias is the control input, and define a family of controllers, the PID-Balancer, from the proportional, integral, and derivative terms of classical PID control. Two widely used methods belong to this family: the auxiliary balancing loss behaves like a proportional controller, and the loss-free bias update is an integral controller acting on the sign of the error. Reading the router as a static map with a one-step delay, we show that the integral term is the only one that both removes persistent imbalance and averages out the step-to-step fluctuation of the measured load, whereas the proportional and derivative terms pass or amplify that fluctuation into the routing bias. We therefore adopt PID (I), the pure-integral member of the family with a single gain. Two settings are evaluated: (i) training an OLMoE backbone from scratch with 16 to 64 experts per layer, through a mid-run mixture switch, and (ii) continual pretraining of the released OLMoE-1B-7B on a new corpus. PID (I) holds balance before the shift and restores it more tightly afterwards than either the auxiliary loss or loss-free balancing, at comparable perplexity.

</details>


### [153] [FondueQA: A Benchmark for Evaluating Parametric Compensation in Multi-Hop QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09274)
*Laurin Schmid,Suman Navaratnarajah,Koel Dutta Chowdhury,Steffen Eger,Josif Grabocka,Christian Frey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 FondueQA 基准，通过将每个多跳事实唯一地定位到指定证据文档，实现可控的文档级证据消融，从而系统研究检索证据不完整时大模型如何用参数化知识进行补偿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多跳基准公开时间久，很可能已被预训练语料污染，导致在缺失证据下答对题时难以区分是"通用参数化知识"还是"对基准实例的记忆"，因而无法干净地研究模型在检索证据不完整时的内部知识利用。

Method: 构建 FondueQA 基准：每个多跳推理所需事实均唯一地定位于特定证据文档，支持文档级证据消融实验（移除桥接文档或答案承载文档等），以观察模型的参数化补偿行为，并考察实体热度与缺失证据结构角色的影响。

Result: 参数化补偿强烈依赖实体热度与缺失证据的结构角色：涉及热门实体的事实补偿更成功；桥接证据有时比答案承载文档本身更有用；模型可能能孤立地回忆缺失事实，但在部分证据下（尤其是上下文含貌似合理的竞争实体时）无法正确使用，Exact Match 最多下降 15 个百分点。

Conclusion: 在无污染、可控消融的设定下，模型的参数化知识补偿是条件性且脆弱的：并非简单"答对即掌握"，而受实体鲜明度与证据结构、干扰实体影响，提示需更细致地评估内部知识的可用性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) show remarkable performance based on parametric knowledge, i.e., their ability to recall facts and associations internalized in a model's weights. However, studying this capability on established multi-hop benchmarks may be complicated by benchmark contamination, as many of these benchmarks have been publicly available long enough to plausibly appear in modern pretraining corpora. As a result, correct answers under missing evidence may reflect either general parametric knowledge or memorization of benchmark-specific instances. To study how models use internal knowledge when retrieval evidence is incomplete, we introduce FondueQA, in which every required multi-hop fact is uniquely localized to a designated evidence document. This enables controlled document-level evidence ablations to study parametric compensation under incomplete retrieval. Our empirical evaluation reveals that parametric compensation strongly depends on both entity popularity and the structural role of missing evidence. Generally, models compensate more successfully for facts involving popular entities. On FondueQA, we show that bridging evidence can be more useful than the answer-bearing document itself. Moreover, models may recall a missing fact in isolation but fail to use it under partial evidence, particularly when the context contains plausible competing entities, which can reduce Exact Match by up to 15 percentage points.

</details>


### [154] [Diversity-Aware Client Selection for Communication-Efficient Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09278)
*Max Halleryd,Sima Sinaei,Viktor Valadi,Mina Alibeigi,Henrik Abrahamsson,Salman Toor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了 Power-of-Choice、Fisher 信息、Centered Kernel Alignment 三种客户端选择策略，并从参与多样性（Gini 系数、KL 散度）视角评估其在 non-IID 联邦学习中的通信效率与泛化表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在隐私敏感、带宽受限场景下有吸引力，但非独立同分布（non-IID）数据下如何在降低通信开销的同时保持模型性能仍是部署难点。客户端选择被认为是同时应对两大挑战的手段，但其对数据多样性与代表性的影响尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 研究三种客户端选择策略：Power-of-Choice、基于 Fisher 信息的选择、基于 Centered Kernel Alignment 的选择；评估指标不仅包括准确率与收敛性，还引入 Gini 系数和 KL 散度量化参与多样性。每轮仅选取 15% 客户端，实验设定为可复现的系统性分析。

Result: 在 non-IID 条件下，每轮仅 15% 客户端参与即可带来显著的通信节省；当选择策略促进多样且均衡的贡献时，其泛化性能优于有偏的选择替代方案。

Conclusion: 以多样性为导向的客户端选择使通信效率与模型质量由相互竞争转为互补目标，为联邦学习的客户端选择设计提供了系统、可复现的实证依据。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed clients without sharing raw data, making it attractive for privacy-sensitive and bandwidth-constrained applications. A practical challenge in deploying FL is reducing communication overhead while maintaining model performance under heterogeneous, non-IID data distributions. Selecting which subset of clients participates in each training round offers a principled way to address both challenges simultaneously, yet its impact on data diversity and representativeness remains underexplored. In this paper, we investigate three client selection strategies, Power-of-Choice, Fisher information, and Centered Kernel Alignment, evaluating them not only by accuracy and convergence, but through the lens of participation diversity using the Gini coefficient and KL divergence. Under non-IID conditions, restricting participation to 15% of clients per round achieves significant communication savings, and when selection promotes diverse and balanced contributions, it also improves generalization over biased alternatives. This work provides a systematic and reproducible analysis demonstrating that diversity-focused client selection renders communication efficiency and model quality complementary rather than competing objectives.

</details>


### [155] [Safety-Focused Reinforcement Learning Using Risk-Sensitive Coupled Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09290)
*Jiabin Lin,Shana Moothedath,Vivek S. Borkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究安全强化学习中的耦合控制问题，将安全约束下的性能优化问题转化为风险敏感与平均奖励准则相结合的问题，并提出双时间尺度Q学习算法求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂环境中运行的系统需要同时实现最优性能与安全性，但现有方法难以兼顾"避免进入危险状态"与"优化平均代价"两个目标，因此需要一种耦合控制框架来处理这一核心挑战。

Method: 将安全定义为阻止系统到达不希望或危险状态；提出以安全优先的耦合控制方法，将原问题通过新颖的问题变换重构为风险敏感问题与平均奖励准则的结合；设计双时间尺度Q学习算法，快时间尺度求解风险敏感控制问题以保证安全，慢时间尺度进行平均代价控制优化性能；并给出算法收敛性的理论保证。

Result: 理论上证明了所提算法的收敛性；在Safety Gymnasium的GridWorld与CarGoal任务上验证了方法性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的双时间尺度耦合控制框架能够在动态环境中实现兼顾安全性与最优性的控制，具有理论收敛保证并得到实验验证。

Abstract: This paper investigates the coupled control problem within safety-focused reinforcement learning, addressing the essential challenge of achieving optimal performance while ensuring safety in systems operating within complex environments. We define safety as the requirement to prevent the system from reaching undesirable or hazardous states. We propose a coupled control approach that prioritizes safety by employing effective methods to prevent the system from entering unsafe states while optimizing the performance metric, defined as the average cost. We propose a novel problem transformation that reformulates it into a risk-sensitive problem combined with an average reward criterion. We present a two-timescale Q-learning algorithm where in the faster timescale, the algorithm solves a risk-sensitive control problem to ensure safety, while on a slower timescale, the algorithm focuses on average cost control. We provide theoretical guarantees for the convergence of our algorithms, establishing their significance in ensuring safe, optimal control in dynamic environments. We validated the performance of our approach using GridWorld and CarGoal task in Safety Gymnasium.

</details>


### [156] [A Stochastic Latent Variable Generalization of Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09302)
*Nikos Giakoumoglou,Paschalis Giakoumoglou,Tania Stathaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: S-JEPA 将 JEPA 中原本固定为确定性 token 的辅助变量 z 实现为真正的随机隐变量（训练时用后验从上下文和目标推断，推理时用上下文条件先验近似），在掩码图像预测中实现无解码器、无重建的随机预测，在不损失表示质量的前提下带来误差相关的预测不确定性与多样化补全能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原 JEPA 蓝图中辅助变量 z 具有重要作用，但此前所有实例化都把 z 退化成一个固定确定性 token，从而把本应多模态的"合理补全分布"坍缩为点估计，无法表达预测不确定性或生成多样补全。

Method: 把 z 建模为随机隐变量：训练阶段由学习到的后验分布从上下文与目标信号推断 z；推理阶段由学习到的、以上下文为条件的先验近似合理补全的分布。整体不含解码器、不做像素重建；其确定性极限下目标函数在函数形式上退化为 I-JEPA 损失（属于对该目标的推广，而非严格包含 I-JEPA 模型）。

Result: 在架构与训练调度匹配的条件下，S-JEPA 优于确定性 I-JEPA 基线，并在同架构下超过 LeJEPA，表示质量无损失；同时额外产生空间结构化的不确定性图与语义多样的补全结果，而确定性预测器均无法给出。

Conclusion: 随机性为 JEPA 带来新能力（误差相关的预测不确定性、多样补全），且不以表示质量为代价，说明将 z 提升为随机隐变量是对 JEPA 框架的有效推广方向。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) predict the latent embedding of a target signal from a context signal, with an auxiliary variable z conditioning the predictor. Despite its prominent role in the original JEPA blueprint, the content of z has been carried by a fixed deterministic token in every prior instantiation, collapsing the genuinely multi-modal distribution over plausible completions to a point estimate. We present S-JEPA, which realises z as a proper stochastic latent variable for masked image prediction: a learned posterior infers z from context and target during training, while a learned context-conditioned prior approximates the distribution of plausible completions at inference, with no decoders and no reconstruction. In its deterministic limit the objective reduces to the I-JEPA loss in functional form, generalising that objective rather than strictly containing the I-JEPA model. Our central finding is that stochasticity adds capabilities, error-correlated predictive uncertainty and diverse completions, at no cost to representation quality: under matched architectures and schedules S-JEPA improves over the deterministic I-JEPA baseline and exceeds LeJEPA at matched architecture, while additionally producing spatially structured uncertainty maps and semantically diverse completions, neither of which the deterministic predictor produces.

</details>


### [157] [Stable probabilistic emulation of partially observed dynamical systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09317)
*Mario Massy,Sara Shamekh,Fabrizio Falasca,Matthieu Blanke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出强制耗散随机插值（forced-dissipative stochastic interpolants）生成框架，用于学习粗粒化部分观测动力系统的随机约化动力学，在结构上保证长期稳定与几何遍历性，并在双时间尺度Lorenz-96系统上验证其对均值与方差的强迫响应预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候（如海气耦合系统）长期预测对决策至关重要，深度学习天气模拟器的成功启发了数据驱动气候建模。但气候模拟面临特殊困难：不可避免的粗粒化引入未解析快变量、需再现长期统计特性、需在任意长rollout及外部扰动下保持稳定。这些需求指向随机数据驱动模拟器，而现代生成模型缺乏稳定性保证。

Method: 提出forced-dissipative stochastic interpolants：一种学习随机约化动力学的生成式框架。其架构引入受湍流动力学启发的结构约束，在设计层面提供稳定性，同时保留stochastic interpolants表征复杂概率动力学的灵活性。借助马尔可夫链稳定性理论，建立Foster–Lyapunov漂移条件，证明模拟器的几何遍历性及稳态外力下的稳定性。

Result: 在双时间尺度Lorenz-96系统（部分观测多尺度湍流的原型）上评估。所得模拟器在长期积分与有限振幅强迫下保持稳定，并能准确预测均值与方差的强迫响应。

Conclusion: 结构约束+生成式随机插值可在保证理论稳定性的同时保持表达力，为数据驱动气候模拟提供兼具长期稳定性与统计保真性的可行路径。

Abstract: Predicting the long-term behavior of partially observed dynamical systems such as the coupled atmosphere-ocean system is central to societal decision-making. Motivated by the success of deep learning emulators in weather forecasting, data-driven climate models represent a promising approach. However, climate emulation poses distinct challenges: models must account for unresolved fast variables introduced by unavoidable coarse-graining, reproduce long-term statistics, and remain stable over arbitrarily long rollouts and under external perturbations. These requirements motivate stochastic data-driven emulators, but modern generative models lack stability guarantees. We introduce forced-dissipative stochastic interpolants, a generative framework for learning stochastic reduced dynamics. Its architecture uses structural constraints inspired by turbulent dynamics, which provide stability by design while retaining the flexibility of stochastic interpolants to represent complex probabilistic dynamics. Building on the theory of Markov-chain stability, we establish a Foster--Lyapunov drift condition and prove geometric ergodicity for our emulator, together with stability under steady external forcing. We evaluate the framework on the two-timescale Lorenz-96 system, a prototype of partially observed multiscale turbulence. The resulting emulator remains stable over long integrations and under finite-amplitude forcing, with accurate prediction of forced responses of both the mean and variance.

</details>


### [158] [DiffusionRef-IMU: Guide-Anchored Residual Diffusion for Cross-Location Inertial Measurement Unit Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09323)
*M. M. Golam Hafiz,Safia Akter Simi,Md. Rakibul Islam,M. F. Mridha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两阶段跨位置IMU信号转换框架DiffusionRef-IMU：先用确定性Direct网络估计目标信号，再用guide-anchored残差扩散模型仅细化剩余误差；16步DDIM在五折交叉验证中平均nRMSE达0.087538，优于所有基线，但零样本外部迁移效果不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴IMU信号随佩戴位置变化而产生跨位置差异，当无法获取目标身体位置的实测信号时，跨位置学习变得困难。论文旨在解决目标位置测量缺失条件下的信号转换问题。

Method: 提出DiffusionRef-IMU两阶段框架：第一阶段由确定性Direct网络给出目标信号初估；第二阶段采用guide-anchored残差扩散模型，仅建模并细化Direct输出与真实目标之间的残差。评估设计包括：五个受试者不重叠、活动-位置组合留出的折（composition-held-out folds）、容量匹配的确定性基线与flow-based残差基线、仅用源数据的活动条件预测、计算消融，以及在两个独立数据集上的真实零样本评估。

Result: 16步确定性DDIM平均nRMSE为0.087538±0.004922，对比Direct的0.090262、确定性残差细化的0.088847、八步CompFlow的0.088417，并在全部五折中优于各基线。4步DDIM在每窗口47.62 ms下仍保持0.087820的nRMSE，具有较优的质量-计算权衡。以仅源数据活动预测器（macro-F1 0.8473）替代真值活动条件后，16步nRMSE仅升至0.088190。真实零样本结果不一：在Grouvel2025上略占优势，但在Saldaña2025上仍劣于Direct。

Conclusion: guide-anchored残差扩散在受控的跨位置重建场景中带来稳定增益，并对活动条件噪声具有一定鲁棒性，4步采样具备实用价值；但在零样本外部数据集迁移上存在明显局限，泛化能力仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Cross-location variation in wearable inertial measurement unit (IMU) signals complicates learning when measurements from a desired target body location are unavailable. This study proposes DiffusionRef-IMU, a two-stage cross-location translation framework in which a deterministic Direct network first estimates the target signal and a guide-anchored residual diffusion model refines only the remaining target discrepancy. The evaluation comprises five subject-disjoint activity-location composition-held-out folds, capacity-matched deterministic and flow-based residual baselines, source-only activity conditioning, computational ablation, and true zero-shot evaluation on two independent datasets. DiffusionRef-IMU with 16-step deterministic DDIM achieved a mean nRMSE of 0.087538 +/- 0.004922, compared with 0.090262 for Direct, 0.088847 for deterministic residual refinement, and 0.088417 for eight-step CompFlow, and outperformed each baseline in all five folds. Four-step DDIM retained a mean nRMSE of 0.087820 at 47.62 ms per window, providing a favourable quality-computation trade-off. Replacing ground-truth activity conditioning with a source-only activity predictor (macro-F1 0.8473) increased 16-step DiffusionRef nRMSE only to 0.088190. True zero-shot evaluation produced mixed results: DiffusionRef was marginally favourable on Grouvel2025 but remained worse than Direct on Saldaña2025. Overall, guide-anchored residual diffusion improves controlled cross-location reconstruction while revealing clear limits in zero-shot external transfer.

</details>


### [159] [Robust Deep Prototype Classifiers via Lipschitz Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09327)
*Adia Khalid Khan,Lukas Gehring,Benjamin Paaßen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文针对深度嵌入的原型分类器（如GLVQ）鲁棒性证书失效问题，证明：若嵌入网络在局部是L-Lipschitz的、且分类头在其嵌入处ε-鲁棒，则整体模型在输入空间是ε/L-鲁棒；并提出基于采样的Lipschitz训练方案，在MNIST、CIFAR-10、Imagenette-160上对比三种分类头与两种嵌入网络的组合，结果表明原型分类头+Lipschitz约束嵌入鲁棒性最强，但由于约束靠采样施加而非构造保证，所得为经验鲁棒性而非严格认证鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒性与可解释性相互关联：受不可感知扰动即改变预测的模型与人类可识别的特征不一致，因而不可解释。原型分类器（如GLVQ）按构造可解释，其假设间隔还给出闭式鲁棒性证书。但在高维数据上，这类浅层模型需要深度嵌入网络才能达到高精度，而一旦引入嵌入网络，间隔证书便失效——间隔只是嵌入空间中距离的陈述，无约束网络可把任意小的输入扰动映射为嵌入空间中任意大的攻击。本文旨在弥合这一缺口。

Method: 理论部分：证明若嵌入网络在给定点附近局部L-Lipschitz，且分类器在其嵌入处ε-鲁棒，则完整模型在输入空间为ε/L-鲁棒。基于该定理提出Lipschitz训练方案，使嵌入网络获得L-Lipschitz性质（通过采样施加约束）。实验部分：将三种分类头（线性、线性SVM、GLVQ）与两种嵌入网络（标准、Lipschitz约束）交叉组合，在MNIST、CIFAR-10、Imagenette-160上，用FGS、PGD与C&W攻击进行评估。

Result: 在三种数据集与三类攻击下的评估表明，原型分类头与Lipschitz约束嵌入的组合取得最强的鲁棒性。这说明Lipschitz约束在原型分类头自身优势之外还带来额外的鲁棒性收益。

Conclusion: 将可解释的原型分类头与Lipschitz约束的嵌入网络结合，可同时兼顾可解释性与鲁棒性，并以理论定理为二者提供衔接。但需注意，由于Lipschitz约束由采样而非构造保证，所报告的鲁棒性是经验性的，而非认证鲁棒性。

Abstract: Robustness and interpretability are interrelated and desirable properties of machine learning models. A model that changes its prediction under imperceptible perturbations behaves inconsistently with human-recognizable features and, hence, is not interpretable. Prototype-based classifiers such as Generalized Learning Vector Quantization address both at once. They are interpretable by construction because they make the decision by comparing the input to the prototypical example; and their hypothesis margin yields a closed form robustness certificate. However, on high-dimensional data, these shallow models need deep embedding networks to achieve high accuracy, and this voids the robustness certificate. The margin is a statement about distances in the embedding space, and an unconstrained network can map an arbitrarily small input perturbation to arbitrarily large attacks in the embedding space. We close this gap with a theorem: if the embedding network is locally L-Lipschitz around a point, and the classifier is ε-robust around its embedding, then the full model is ε/L-robust in the input space. Based on this, we propose a Lipschitz training scheme to achieve L-Lipschitz properties for embedding networks. We evaluate three classification heads (linear, linear Support Vector Machine, and GLVQ) crossed with two embedding networks (standard and Lipschitz-constrained) on MNIST, CIFAR-10, and Imagenette-160, under FGS, PGD and C\&W attacks. The evaluation results show that combining a prototype-based head with a Lipschitz-constrained embedding provides the strongest robustness. This suggests that the Lipschitz constraint provides an additional robustness benefit in addition to the advantage provided by the prototype-based head. Since the Lipschitz constraint is enforced by sampling rather than by construction, the reported robustness is empirical rather than certified.

</details>


### [160] [Probably Safe Sim-to-Real Transfer with Lifetime Validity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09344)
*Rohan Tangri,Jan-Peter Calliess*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Dejavu 方法，在约束马尔可夫决策过程中实现强化学习策略 sim-to-real 迁移的"概率安全"，安全保证在无界次数的策略更新序列上持续成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真到现实（sim-to-real）迁移的强化学习策略存在仿真与现实不匹配的风险，已有方法缺乏在整个持续策略更新过程中都成立的安全性保证。

Method: 将安全问题视为对候选策略安全性的检验：利用逐决策重要性采样（per-decision importance sampling）与投注置信序列（betting confidence sequence）构造 anytime valid 检验，在部署前仅用生产环境中安全基线策略采集的轨迹对每个仿真训练出的候选策略进行认证；仿真质量不影响安全性保证，只影响生产样本效率；并通过可求和的逐次检验失败概率实现 lifetime valid 性质。

Result: 在三个 sim-to-real 基准上取得实证结果，支持上述理论结论。

Conclusion: Dejavu 为约束 MDP 中的 sim-to-real 策略迁移提供了在无界策略更新序列下仍然成立的概率安全保证，且该保证对仿真质量不敏感。

Abstract: We present Dejavu, a method that makes sim-to-real transfer of reinforcement learning policies in constrained Markov decision processes probably safe over an unbounded sequence of policy updates. An anytime valid test using per-decision importance sampling and a betting confidence sequence certifies each candidate trained in simulation before deployment using only trajectories collected by a safe baseline policy operating in production. Simulation quality does not affect the safety guarantee, only the production sample efficiency of the method. Finally, a summable per-test failure probability yields the lifetime valid property. Empirical results across three sim-to-real benchmarks support the theoretical work.

</details>


### [161] [Reliable Prerequisite-Aware Learning Path Recommendation under Uncertain Knowledge Structures: Long-Horizon Evaluation and Cross-Dataset Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09345)
*M. M. Golam Hafiz,A. K. M. Shahriar Emtiaz,Safia Akter Simi,Md. Rakibul Islam,M. F. Mridha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个以可靠性为导向、感知先修结构的推荐框架，在MOOCCubeX做确认性评估并用Junyi做独立外部验证，联合考察下一步资源排序、不确定的先修结构、硬/软/混合约束语义、图鲁棒性与自回归长程路径生成。结果显示：即时排序精度、先修结构可靠性与长程路径质量应作为互补而非可互换的评价维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习路径推荐依赖知识结构与先修关系，但其可靠性本身不确定；且"下一步资源"预测的高精度并不能保证长程路径行为可靠。因此需要把即时排序精度、先修结构可靠性与长程路径质量分开、并作为互补维度来评估。

Method: 构建可靠性导向的先修感知框架，联合考察：即时下一步资源排序、不确定先修结构、硬约束/软约束/混合约束语义、图鲁棒性、以及自回归路径生成。开发阶段使用3,187门MOOCCubeX课程；随后在541门留出课程、37,747条转移上做冻结的确认性评估；并用Junyi（层级元数据而非显式先修图，非零样本迁移）做独立的外部结构/层级感知验证；另做受控图变体消融（含无图条件）。

Result: MOOCCubeX冻结评估中，XGBoost pairwise为最强即时排序器（MRR=0.9048），所提先修感知图模型为0.7707；图变体消融中无图条件即时排序最好，即时预测强烈依赖可用的标题/路径相似度信息；自回归评估揭示了单步排序无法体现的路径性质。Junyi上Markov取得最强MRR=0.4453，所提图模型为0.3954，各排序指标差异具有指标特异性。

Conclusion: 在教育推荐中，即时预测精度、先修结构可靠性与长程路径质量应被视为互补而非可互换的评价维度；单步排序的高精度不足以支撑对长程路径可靠性的判断。

Abstract: Learning-path recommendation often depends on knowledge and prerequisite structures whose reliability is uncertain, while high accuracy in predicting the next resource does not necessarily establish reliable long-horizon path behaviour. This study develops a reliability-oriented prerequisite-aware framework that jointly examines immediate next-resource ranking, uncertain prerequisite structures, hard, soft and hybrid constraint semantics, graph robustness, and autoregressive path generation under course-level confirmatory evaluation and independent cross-dataset validation. Development used 3,187 MOOCCubeX courses, followed by a frozen confirmatory evaluation on 541 held-out courses and 37,747 transitions. The primary MOOCCubeX task is structural and course-prefix-conditioned rather than learner-personalised. Junyi provides independent structure-/hierarchy-aware external validation, uses hierarchy metadata rather than an explicit prerequisite graph and is not a zero-shot transfer setting. On the frozen MOOCCubeX evaluation, XGBoost pairwise was the strongest observed immediate ranker, with mean reciprocal rank (MRR) of 0.9048, whereas the proposed prerequisite-aware graph model achieved 0.7707. In the controlled graph-variant development ablation, the no-graph condition produced the strongest immediate ranking result, and immediate prediction showed strong dependence on permitted title/path-similarity information. Autoregressive evaluation exposed path properties not captured by one-step ranking. On Junyi, Markov achieved the strongest observed MRR of 0.4453, compared with 0.3954 for the proposed graph model, with metric-specific differences across ranking measures.
